booked = []

for _ in range(n):
    s, d = map(int, input().split())
    preferred_start = s
    preferred_end = s + d - 1
    available = True
    for a, b in booked:
        if not (preferred_end < a or preferred_start > b):
            available = False
            break
    if available:
        new_interval = (preferred_start, preferred_end)
        inserted = False
        for j in range(len(booked)):
            if booked[j][0] > new_interval[0]:
                booked.insert(j, new_interval)
                inserted = True
                break
        if not inserted:
            booked.append(new_interval)
        print(preferred_start, preferred_end)
    else:
        if not booked:
            x = 1
            new_end = x + d - 1
            booked.append((x, new_end))
            print(x, new_end)
            continue
        gaps = []
        first_a, first_b = booked[0]
        if first_a > 1:
            gaps.append((1, first_a - 1))
        for i in range(len(booked) - 1):
            a_prev, b_prev = booked[i]
            a_next, b_next = booked[i + 1]
            if b_prev + 1 <= a_next - 1:
                gaps.append((b_prev + 1, a_next - 1))
        last_b = booked[-1][1]
        gaps.append((last_b + 1, float('inf')))
        found = False
        for gap_start, gap_end in gaps:
            if gap_end == float('inf') or (gap_end - gap_start + 1) >= d:
                x = gap_start
                new_end = x + d - 1
                if gap_end != float('inf') and new_end > gap_end:
                    continue
                new_interval = (x, new_end)
                inserted = False
                for j in range(len(booked)):
                    if booked[j][0] > x:
                        booked.insert(j, new_interval)
                        inserted = True
                        break
                if not inserted:
                    booked.append(new_interval)
                print(x, new_end)
                found = True
                break
        if not found:
            x = last_b + 1
            new_end = x + d - 1
            booked.append((x, new_end))

import math

n, L, R = map(int, input().split())
MOD = 10**9 + 7

# Compute derangements D[0..n]
D = [0] * (n + 1)
D[0] = 1
if n >= 1:
    D[1] = 0
for i in range(2, n + 1):
    D[i] = (i - 1) * (D[i - 1] + D[i - 2])

sum_total = 0
for x in range(L, R + 1):
    sum_total += math.comb(n, x) * D[x]

fact_n = math.factorial(n)
result = (sum_total * pow(fact_n, MOD - 2, MOD)) % MOD
print(result)<#sep#>-

def main():
    import sys
    input = sys.stdin.read().split()
    idx = 0
    T = int(input[idx])
    idx += 1
    for _ in range(T):
        N = int(input[idx])
        K = int(input[idx + 1])
        idx += 2
        if K > 81:
            print(-1)
            continue
        index = K - 1
        a = (index // 9) + 1
        idx_in_group = index % 9
        sorted_b = []
        if 0 != a:
            sorted_b.append(0)
        for x in range(1, 10):
            if x != a:
                sorted_b.append(x)
        b = sorted_b[idx_in_group]
        res = []
        for i in range(N):
            if i % 2 == 0:
                res.append(str(a))
            else:
                res.append(str(b))
        print(''.join(res))

MOD = 998244353
MAX = 10**6  # Adjust based on constraints to avoid MLE, but 1e6 is manageable.

# Precompute factorial, inverse factorial, and inverses up to MAX
fact = [1] * (MAX)
for i in range(1, MAX):
    fact[i] = fact[i-1] * i % MOD

inv_fact = [1] * (MAX)
inv_fact[MAX-1] = pow(fact[MAX-1], MOD-2, MOD)
for i in range(MAX-2, -1, -1):
    inv_fact[i] = inv_fact[i+1] * (i+1) % MOD

def C(n, k):
    if n < 0 or k < 0 or n < k:
        return 0
    return fact[n] * inv_fact[k] % MOD * inv_fact[n - k] % MOD

p, s, r = map(int, input().split())

# Precompute inverses for 1..p
max_inv = p
inv = [0] * (max_inv + 2)
for i in range(1, max_inv + 1):
    inv[i] = pow(i, MOD-2, MOD)

numerator = 0

for a1 in range(r, s + 1):
    sum_other = s - a1
    if sum_other < 0:
        continue
    for k in range(0, p):
        required_sum = sum_other - a1 * k
        m = (p - 1) - k
        if required_sum < 0 or m < 0:
            continue
        if a1 == 0:
            if required_sum == 0 and m == 0:
                ways = 1
            else:
                ways = 0
        else:
            c = a1 - 1
            ways = 0
            max_i = required_sum // (c + 1) if (c + 1) != 0 else 0
            max_i = min(m, max_i)
            for i in range(0, max_i + 1):
                rem = required_sum - i * (c + 1)
                if rem < 0:
                    continue
                comb_i = C(m, i)
                comb_rem = C(rem + m - 1, m - 1)
                if i % 2 == 0:
                    ways = (ways + comb_i * comb_rem) % MOD
                else:
                    ways = (ways - comb_i * comb_rem) % MOD
            ways %= MOD
        comb_k = C(p-1, k)
        inv_k_plus_1 = inv[k + 1]
        contribution = comb_k * ways % MOD
        contribution = contribution * inv_k_plus_1 % MOD
        numerator = (numerator + contribution) % MOD

denominator = C(s - r + p - 1, p - 1)
if denominator == 0:
    print(0)
else:
    result = numerator * pow(denominator, MOD-2, MOD) % MOD
    print(result)<#sep#>-

MOD = 10**9 + 7

n = int(input())

if n < 2:
    print(0)
else:
    # Compute factorial(n) mod MOD
    fact = 1
    for i in range(2, n + 1):
        fact = (fact * i) % MOD
    
    # Compute 2^(n-1) mod MOD
    pow2 = pow(2, n - 1, MOD)
    
    # Calculate the result
    result = (fact - pow2) % MOD
    print(result)<#sep#>-

def comb2(n):
    return n * (n - 1) // 2 if n >= 2 else 0

def compute(s, A, B, C):
    if s < 0:
        return 0
    a = A + 1
    b_val = B + 1
    c_val = C + 1

    c0 = comb2(s + 2)
    
    overA = comb2(s - a + 2) if s >= a else 0
    overB = comb2(s - b_val + 2) if s >= b_val else 0
    overC = comb2(s - c_val + 2) if s >= c_val else 0
    sum1 = overA + overB + overC

    overAB = comb2(s - a - b_val + 2) if s >= (a + b_val) else 0
    overAC = comb2(s - a - c_val + 2) if s >= (a + c_val) else 0
    overBC = comb2(s - b_val - c_val + 2) if s >= (b_val + c_val) else 0
    sum2 = overAB + overAC + overBC

    overABC = comb2(s - a - b_val - c_val + 2) if s >= (a + b_val + c_val) else 0
    sum3 = overABC

    total = c0 - sum1 + sum2 - sum3
    return max(total, 0)

t = int(input())
for _ in range(t):
    N, A, B, C = map(int, input().split())
    K = min(N, A + B + C)
    res = 0
    for s in range(K + 1):
        res += compute(s, A, B, C)
    print(res)<#sep#>-

from collections import defaultdict

def find_lex_smallest_A(S):
    # Calculate the required counts for each character in A
    char_count = defaultdict(int)
    for c in S:
        char_count[c] += 1
    required = {k: v // 2 for k, v in char_count.items()}
    
    # Precompute suffix counts for each position
    n = len(S)
    suffix_counts = [defaultdict(int) for _ in range(n + 1)]
    for i in range(n - 1, -1, -1):
        # Copy previous counts
        for key in suffix_counts[i + 1]:
            suffix_counts[i][key] = suffix_counts[i + 1][key]
        # Update current character count
        current_char = S[i]
        suffix_counts[i][current_char] += 1
    
    stack = []
    current_counts = defaultdict(int)
    
    for i in range(n):
        c = S[i]
        if current_counts[c] >= required[c]:
            continue  # Already met the required count for this character
        
        # Try to pop the stack to get a lex larger sequence
        while stack:
            top_char = stack[-1]
            if top_char >= c:
                break
            # Check if we can remove the top character
            needed = required[top_char] - (current_counts[top_char] - 1)
            if suffix_counts[i].get(top_char, 0) >= needed:
                stack.pop()
                current_counts[top_char] -= 1
            else:
                break
        
        # Add current character to the stack if possible
        if current_counts[c] < required[c]:
            stack.append(c)
            current_counts[c] += 1
    
    # The lex smallest A is the reverse of the constructed R
    return ''.join(reversed(stack))

# Read input and output the result
S = input().strip()
print(find_lex_smallest_A(S))<#sep#>-

import sys

def main():
    while True:
        line = sys.stdin.readline()
        if not line:
            break
        m = int(line.strip())
        if m == 0:
            break
        # Read constellation points
        constellation = []
        for _ in range(m):
            x, y = map(int, sys.stdin.readline().split())
            constellation.append((x, y))
        # Read photo points
        n = int(sys.stdin.readline())
        photo = []
        photo_set = set()
        for _ in range(n):
            x, y = map(int, sys.stdin.readline().split())
            photo.append((x, y))
            photo_set.add((x, y))
        # Find the translation
        c0 = constellation[0]
        dx, dy = 0, 0
        found = False
        for p in photo:
            dx_candidate = p[0] - c0[0]
            dy_candidate = p[1] - c0[1]
            valid = True
            for c in constellation:
                tx = c[0] + dx_candidate
                ty = c[1] + dy_candidate
                if (tx, ty) not in photo_set:
                    valid = False
                    break
            if valid:
                dx, dy = dx_candidate, dy_candidate
                found = True
                break
        if found:
            print(f"{dx} {dy}")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()<#sep#>-

v, e, r = map(int, input().split())
edges = []
for _ in range(e):
    s, t, w = map(int, input().split())
    edges.append((s, t, w))

def find_min_arborescence(nodes, edges, root):
    if len(nodes) == 1:
        return 0

    min_incoming = {}
    for node in nodes:
        if node == root:
            continue
        incoming = [(s, t, w) for s, t, w in edges if t == node]
        if not incoming:
            return float('inf')
        min_edge = min(incoming, key=lambda x: x[2])
        min_incoming[node] = min_edge

    visited = set()
    cycle = []
    for node in nodes:
        if node == root or node in visited:
            continue
        path = []
        current = node
        while True:
            if current in path:
                idx = path.index(current)
                cycle = path[idx:]
                break
            path.append(current)
            if current not in min_incoming:
                break
            parent = min_incoming[current][0]
            current = parent
            if current == root or current in visited:
                break
        if cycle:
            break
        visited.update(path)
    if not cycle:
        return sum(edge[2] for edge in min_incoming.values())

    cycle_sum = sum(min_incoming[node][2] for node in cycle)
    new_node = max(nodes) + 1
    new_nodes = [n for n in nodes if n not in cycle] + [new_node]
    new_edges = []
    for (s, t, w) in edges:
        s_in = s in cycle
        t_in = t in cycle
        if s_in and t_in:
            continue
        elif not s_in and not t_in:
            new_edges.append((s, t, w))
        elif not s_in and t_in:
            min_w = min_incoming[t][2]
            new_edges.append((s, new_node, w - min_w))
        else:
            new_edges.append((new_node, t, w))
    new_root = new_node if root in cycle else root
    contracted_sum = find_min_arborescence(new_nodes, new_edges, new_root)
    return cycle_sum + contracted_sum

nodes = list(range(v))
print(find_min_arborescence(nodes, edges, r))<#sep#>-

import heapq
from collections import defaultdict

def main():
    n, m, k, c, d = map(int, input().split())
    a = list(map(int, input().split()))
    streets = []
    for _ in range(m):
        x, y = map(int, input().split())
        streets.append((x, y))
    
    directed_edges = defaultdict(list)
    for x, y in streets:
        directed_edges[x].append(y)
        directed_edges[y].append(x)
    
    edge_counts = defaultdict(int)
    total_discontent = 0
    T_max = 200  # Sufficiently large time steps
    
    def find_path(start):
        INF = float('inf')
        dist = defaultdict(lambda: INF)
        prev = dict()
        heap = []
        start_node = (start, 0)
        heapq.heappush(heap, (0, start, 0))
        dist[start_node] = 0
        min_total_cost = INF
        best_node = None
        
        while heap:
            current_cost, u, t = heapq.heappop(heap)
            if u == 1:
                total_cost = current_cost + c * t
                if total_cost < min_total_cost:
                    min_total_cost = total_cost
                    best_node = (u, t)
                continue
            if t >= T_max:
                continue
            if current_cost > dist[(u, t)]:
                continue
            # Waiting edge
            if u != 1:
                next_t = t + 1
                next_node = (u, next_t)
                new_cost = current_cost
                if new_cost < dist[next_node]:
                    dist[next_node] = new_cost
                    prev[next_node] = (u, t, 'wait', None, None)
                    heapq.heappush(heap, (new_cost, u, next_t))
            # Movement edges
            for v in directed_edges.get(u, []):
                edge_key = (u, v, t)
                current_count = edge_counts.get(edge_key, 0)
                edge_cost = d * (2 * current_count + 1)
                new_cost = current_cost + edge_cost
                next_t = t + 1
                next_node = (v, next_t)
                if new_cost < dist[next_node]:
                    dist[next_node] = new_cost
                    prev[next_node] = (u, t, 'move', u, v)
                    heapq.heappush(heap, (new_cost, v, next_t))
        
        # Find best arrival time
        min_total = INF
        best_node = None
        for t in range(T_max + 1):
            node = (1, t)
            if dist[node] != INF:
                total_cost = dist[node] + c * t
                if total_cost < min_total:
                    min_total = total_cost
                    best_node = node
        
        if best_node is None:
            return (INF, [])
        
        # Reconstruct path
        path_edges = []
        current_node = best_node
        while current_node != start_node:
            u, t = current_node
            if current_node not in prev:
                break
            prev_info = prev[current_node]
            prev_u, prev_t, action, edge_u, edge_v = prev_info
            if action == 'move':
                path_edges.append((edge_u, edge_v, prev_t))
            current_node = (prev_u, prev_t)
        path_edges.reverse()
        return (min_total, path_edges)
    
    for person in a:
        min_total, path = find_path(person)
        for u, v, t in path:
            edge_key = (u, v, t)
            edge_counts[edge_key] += 1
        total_discontent += min_total
    
    print(total_discontent)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()<#sep#>-

from collections import defaultdict

def main():
    n = input().strip()
    digits = list(n)
    freq = defaultdict(int)
    for d in digits:
        freq[d] += 1

    max_k = 0
    best = None

    for k in range(len(digits), 0, -1):
        f = freq.copy()
        pairs = []
        possible = True

        found = False
        for d1 in '9876543210':
            d2 = str(10 - int(d1))
            if d1 > d2:
                continue
            if d1 == d2:
                if f[d1] >= 2:
                    pairs.append((d1, d2))
                    f[d1] -= 2
                    found = True
                    break
            else:
                if f[d1] >= 1 and f[d2] >= 1:
                    pairs.append((d1, d2))
                    f[d1] -= 1
                    f[d2] -= 1
                    found = True
                    break
        if not found:
            continue

        for _ in range(k-1):
            found = False
            for d1 in '9876543210':
                d2 = str(9 - int(d1))
                if f[d1] >= 1 and f[d2] >= 1:
                    pairs.append((d1, d2))
                    f[d1] -= 1
                    f[d2] -= 1
                    found = True
                    break
            if not found:
                possible = False
                break
        if not possible:
            continue

        for d in f:
            if f[d] % 2 != 0:
                possible = False
                break
        if not possible:
            continue

        remaining = []
        for d, cnt in f.items():
            remaining.extend([d] * (cnt // 2))
            remaining.extend([d] * (cnt // 2))

        a_tail = []
        b_tail = []
        for p in reversed(pairs):
            a_tail.append(p[0])
            b_tail.append(p[1])

        remaining_sorted = sorted(remaining, reverse=True)
        a_head = []
        b_head = []
        for i in range(0, len(remaining_sorted), 2):
            a_head.append(remaining_sorted[i])
            if i+1 < len(remaining_sorted):
                b_head.append(remaining_sorted[i+1])
            else:
                b_head.append(remaining_sorted[i])

        a = a_head + a_tail
        b = b_head + b_tail

        if a[0] == '0' and len(a) > 1:
            for i in range(1, len(a)):
                if a[i] != '0':
                    a[0], a[i] = a[i], a[0]
                    break
        if b[0] == '0' and len(b) > 1:
            for i in range(1, len(b)):
                if b[i] != '0':
                    b[0], b[i] = b[i], b[0]
                    break

        if k > max_k:
            max_k = k
            best = (a, b)
            break

    if best is None:
        s = sorted(digits, reverse=True)
        a = s
        b = s.copy()
        best = (a, b)

    print(''.join(best[0]))
    print(''.join(best[1]))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()<#sep#>-

import sys
from collections import deque

def main():
    while True:
        line = sys.stdin.readline()
        while line.strip() == '':
            line = sys.stdin.readline()
        x, y = map(int, line.strip().split())
        if x == 0 and y == 0:
            break
        exchange_map = {}
        for _ in range(y):
            parts = sys.stdin.readline().strip().split()
            while len(parts) < 2:
                parts += sys.stdin.readline().strip().split()
            p, q = parts
            p_line = int(p[:-1])
            p_end = p[-1]
            q_line = int(q[:-1])
            q_end = q[-1]
            if (p_line, p_end) not in exchange_map:
                exchange_map[(p_line, p_end)] = []
            exchange_map[(p_line, p_end)].append((q_line, q_end))
            if (q_line, q_end) not in exchange_map:
                exchange_map[(q_line, q_end)] = []
            exchange_map[(q_line, q_end)].append((p_line, p_end))
        initial = []
        for _ in range(x):
            s = sys.stdin.readline().strip()
            initial.append(s.replace('-', ''))
        target = []
        for _ in range(x):
            t = sys.stdin.readline().strip()
            target.append(t.replace('-', ''))
        initial_state = tuple(initial)
        target_state = tuple(target)

        def is_goal(state):
            for i in range(x):
                curr = state[i]
                targ = target[i]
                if curr != targ and curr[::-1] != targ:
                    return False
            return True

        if is_goal(initial_state):
            print(0)
            continue

        visited = set()
        queue = deque([(initial_state, 0)])
        visited.add(initial_state)
        found = False

        while queue and not found:
            state, steps = queue.popleft()
            for line_idx in range(x):
                current_line = state[line_idx]
                if not current_line:
                    continue
                n = len(current_line)
                for split in range(n + 1):
                    left = current_line[:split]
                    right = current_line[split:]
                    if left:
                        current_end = 'W'
                        if (line_idx, current_end) in exchange_map:
                            for (j, j_end) in exchange_map[(line_idx, current_end)]:
                                new_j = state[j]
                                new_j_part = left + new_j if j_end == 'W' else new_j + left
                                new_state = list(state)
                                new_state[line_idx] = right
                                new_state[j] = new_j_part
                                new_state = tuple(new_state)
                                if new_state not in visited:
                                    if is_goal(new_state):
                                        print(steps + 1)
                                        found = True
                                        queue = deque()
                                        break
                                    visited.add(new_state)
                                    queue.append((new_state, steps + 1))
                            if found:
                                break
                        if found:
                            break
                    if right:
                        current_end = 'E'
                        if (line_idx, current_end) in exchange_map:
                            for (j, j_end) in exchange_map[(line_idx, current_end)]:
                                new_j = state[j]
                                new_j_part = right + new_j if j_end == 'W' else new_j + right
                                new_state = list(state)
                                new_state[line_idx] = left
                                new_state[j] = new_j_part
                                new_state = tuple(new_state)
                                if new_state not in visited:
                                    if is_goal(new_state):
                                        print(steps + 1)
                                        found = True
                                        queue = deque()
                                        break
                                    visited.add(new_state)
                                    queue.append((new_state, steps + 1))
                            if found:
                                break
                        if found:
                            break
                if found:
                    break
            if found:
                break

if __name__ == "__main__":

import sys
from collections import deque

def main():
    n, m = map(int, sys.stdin.readline().split())
    d = list(map(int, sys.stdin.readline().split()))
    edges = []
    for i in range(m):
        u, v = map(int, sys.stdin.readline().split())
        edges.append((u-1, v-1))
    
    constrained = [i for i in range(n) if d[i] != -1]
    sum_required = sum(d[i] for i in constrained) % 2
    count_unconstrained = n - len(constrained)
    
    if count_unconstrained == 0 and sum_required % 2 == 1:
        print(-1)
        return
    
    adj = [[] for _ in range(n)]
    for idx, (u, v) in enumerate(edges):
        adj[u].append((v, idx))
        adj[v].append((u, idx))
    
    visited = [False] * n
    parent = [-1] * n
    parent_edge = [-1] * n
    
    root = 0
    for i in range(n):
        if d[i] == -1:
            root = i
            break
    
    q = deque([root])
    visited[root] = True
    while q:
        u = q.popleft()
        for v, e in adj[u]:
            if not visited[v]:
                visited[v] = True
                parent[v] = u
                parent_edge[v] = e
                q.append(v)
    
    selected = [False] * m
    degree = [0] * n
    
    stack = []
    post_order = []
    visited = [False] * n
    visited[root] = True
    stack.append(root)
    while stack:
        u = stack.pop()
        post_order.append(u)
        for v, e in adj[u]:
            if parent[v] == u and not visited[v]:
                visited[v] = True
                stack.append(v)
    post_order = post_order[::-1]
    
    for u in post_order:
        if u == root:
            continue
        req = d[u] if d[u] != -1 else 0
        if (degree[u] % 2) != req:
            e = parent_edge[u]
            selected[e] = not selected[e]
            degree[u] += 1
            degree[parent[u]] += 1
    
    if d[root] != -1 and (degree[root] % 2) != d[root]:
        print(-1)
        return
    
    cross_count = 0
    for i in range(m):
        if selected[i]:
            u, v = edges[i]
            if (d[u] == -1) ^ (d[v] == -1):
                cross_count += 1
    
    if (sum_required + cross_count) % 2 != 0:
        found = False
        for i in range(m):
            u, v = edges[i]
            if (d[u] == -1) ^ (d[v] == -1):
                if parent_edge[u] != i and parent_edge[v] != i:
                    selected[i] = not selected[i]
                    found = True
                    break
        if not found:
            print(-1)
            return
    
    result = [i+1 for i in range(m) if selected[i]]
    print(len(result))
    for x in result:
        print(x)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()<#sep#>-

MOD = 10**9 + 7

def main():
    s = input().strip()
    if not s:
        print(1)
        return
    
    # Split into runs
    runs = []
    current = s[0]
    count = 1
    for c in s[1:]:
        if c == current:
            count += 1
        else:
            runs.append((current, count))
            current = c
            count = 1
    runs.append((current, count))
    
    # DP: dictionary with keys (last_char, parity), value is count
    dp = {}
    # Initialize with the first run
    first_char, first_len = runs[0]
    even = first_len // 2
    odd = first_len % 2
    # Possible transitions for the first run
    # Original: if even, it can be reduced to even//2 pairs, but how?
    # Wait, the run can be processed into any combination of transformations.
    # For a run of char c with length l:
    # The number of possible ways to leave it as c with parity p (0 or 1)
    # and add ways where it's transformed into d with parity p.
    # For example, even l for c: can be transformed into d's l//2, which can be even or odd.
    # But this is complicated. Let's use the approach from the contest solution.
    
    # For a run of c with length l:
    # The possible states after processing are:
    # (c, l % 2) --> keep all as c
    # (d, k % 1) where k is the number of replacements. But this is not straightforward.
    # Alternative approach: for a run of c with length l, the possible states after processing are:
    # 1. (c, l mod 2) --> ways: 1 (if no replacements)
    # 2. (d, m) where m depends on the number of replacements.
    # But how to compute the number of ways.
    # According to the contest solution, the number of states is based on the parity and the possible transitions.
    # The key is that each run can generate transitions based on its parity.
    
    # So for a run of c, length l:
    # The number of ways to end with c is 1 (if we do not merge with previous)
    # and for d it's based on the number of possible replacements.
    # Alternatively, the possible transitions for a run are:
    # - stay as c with parity l%2: ways = 1
    # - switch to d with parity (l // 2) % 2: ways = 1 (if l >= 2)
    # But this is not accurate.
    # Maybe for a run of c with length l:
    # The number of ways to have the last character as c with parity l mod 2 is 1.
    # The number of ways to have the last character as d with parity (l // 2) % 2 is 1 if l >= 1.
    # But this seems to be the case for certain conditions.
    
    # The correct approach from the contest solution is to model transitions for each run:
    # For a run of c with length l, the possible ways to combine with previous state:
    # - If the previous state is (c_prev, p_prev), then the current run can generate new states.
    # For each possible state (c_prev, p_prev), and for the current run of (c_current, l):
    # Compute the possible new states after processing the current run.
    # The current run can generate:
    # 1. If processed to be kept as c_current with parity l%2: ways = 1
    # 2. If processed to be turned into d (the other character) with parity (l//2) % 2: ways = 1 if l >= 1
    
    # So for each run, the possible new states are:
    new_dp = {}
    c, l = runs[0]
    other = 'a' if c == 'b' else 'b'
    # original processing: end with c, parity l%2, ways += 1
    # transformed processing: end with other, parity (l//2)%2, ways += 1 if l >= 1
    new_dp[(c, l % 2)] = new_dp.get((c, l % 2), 0) + 1
    if l >= 1:
        new_dp[(other, (l // 2) % 2)] = new_dp.get((other, (l // 2) % 2), 0) + 1
    
    dp = new_dp
    
    for i in range(1, len(runs)):
        current_char, current_len = runs[i]
        other_char = 'a' if current_char == 'b' else 'b'
        new_dp = {}
        # For each state in the current dp, combine with the current run
        for (prev_char, prev_p), prev_count in dp.items():
            # Process the current run with possible transitions
            # For the current run, two possibilities: kept as current_char or transformed into other_char
            
            # Case 1: kept as current_char
            kept_p = current_len % 2
            if prev_char == current_char:
                # Merge the parity: prev_p + kept_p
                total_p = (prev_p + kept_p) % 2
                new_char = current_char
                new_dp[(new_char, total_p)] = (new_dp.get((new_char, total_p), 0) + prev_count) % MOD
            else:
                # Add the current run as a new segment
                new_dp[(current_char, kept_p)] = (new_dp.get((current_char, kept_p), 0) + prev_count) % MOD
            
            # Case 2: transformed into other_char
            # Only possible if current_len >= 1?
            # The number of times we can replace is floor(current_len / 2), leading to other_char occurrences
            transformed_p = (current_len // 2) % 2
            # transformed_char is other_char
            transformed_char = other_char
            if prev_char == transformed_char:
                # Merge parity
                total_p = (prev_p + transformed_p) % 2
                new_dp[(transformed_char, total_p)] = (new_dp.get((transformed_char, total_p), 0) + prev_count) % MOD
            else:
                new_dp[(transformed_char, transformed_p)] = (new_dp.get((transformed_char, transformed_p), 0) + prev_count) % MOD
        
        dp = new_dp
    
    # Sum all possible states in the final dp
    total = sum(dp.values()) % MOD
    print(total)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()<#sep#>-

import math

def is_in_sector(cx, cy, px, py, wind_dir, a, d_angle):
    dx = px - cx
    dy = py - cy
    distance_sq = dx**2 + dy**2
    if distance_sq > a*a:
        return False
    theta = math.degrees(math.atan2(dy, dx)) % 360
    delta = abs(theta - wind_dir)
    delta %= 360
    if delta > 180:
        delta = 360 - delta
    return delta <= d_angle / 2

def main():
    import sys
    data = sys.stdin.read().split()
    ptr = 0
    while True:
        if ptr >= len(data):
            break
        H = int(data[ptr])
        R = int(data[ptr+1])
        ptr += 2
        if H == 0 and R == 0:
            break
        
        houses = []
        for _ in range(H):
            hx = int(data[ptr])
            hy = int(data[ptr+1])
            houses.append((hx, hy))
            ptr += 2
        
        U, M, S, du, dm, ds = map(int, data[ptr:ptr+6])
        ptr += 6
        
        other_plums = []
        for _ in range(U):
            x = int(data[ptr])
            y = int(data[ptr+1])
            other_plums.append((x, y))
            ptr += 2
        
        peaches = []
        for _ in range(M):
            x = int(data[ptr])
            y = int(data[ptr+1])
            peaches.append((x, y))
            ptr += 2
        
        cherries = []
        for _ in range(S):
            x = int(data[ptr])
            y = int(data[ptr+1])
            cherries.append((x, y))
            ptr += 2
        
        winds = []
        for _ in range(R):
            w = int(data[ptr])
            a = int(data[ptr+1])
            winds.append((w, a))
            ptr += 2
        
        other_flowers = []
        for x, y in other_plums:
            other_flowers.append((x, y, du))
        for x, y in peaches:
            other_flowers.append((x, y, dm))
        for x, y in cherries:
            other_flowers.append((x, y, ds))
        
        max_count = -1
        counts = []
        for hx, hy in houses:
            count = 0
            for w, a in winds:
                if not is_in_sector(0, 0, hx, hy, w, a, du):
                    continue
                valid = True
                for ox, oy, oa in other_flowers:
                    if is_in_sector(ox, oy, hx, hy, w, a, oa):
                        valid = False
                        break
                if valid:
                    count += 1
            counts.append(count)
            if count > max_count:
                max_count = count
        
        if max_count <= 0:
            print("NA")
        else:
            result = [str(i+1) for i, cnt in enumerate(counts) if cnt == max_count]
            print(" ".join(result))

from collections import deque

def main():
    import sys
    input = sys.stdin.read
    data = input().split()
    idx = 0
    N = int(data[idx]); idx +=1
    M = int(data[idx]); idx +=1
    K = int(data[idx]); idx +=1
    
    bikers = []
    for _ in range(N):
        x = int(data[idx]); idx +=1
        y = int(data[idx]); idx +=1
        bikers.append((x, y))
    
    bikes = []
    for _ in range(M):
        x = int(data[idx]); idx +=1
        y = int(data[idx]); idx +=1
        bikes.append((x, y))
    
    # Precompute squared distances
    dist_sq = []
    for bx, by in bikers:
        row = []
        for mx, my in bikes:
            dx = bx - mx
            dy = by - my
            row.append(dx*dx + dy*dy)
        dist_sq.append(row)
    
    max_d = max(max(row) for row in dist_sq)
    low = 0
    high = max_d
    answer = high
    
    def hopcroft_karp(graph, num_left, num_right):
        pair_u = [-1] * num_left
        pair_v = [-1] * num_right
        dist = [0] * num_left
        
        def bfs():
            queue = deque()
            for u in range(num_left):
                if pair_u[u] == -1:
                    dist[u] = 0
                    queue.append(u)
                else:
                    dist[u] = float('inf')
            dist_null = float('inf')
            while queue:
                u = queue.popleft()
                if dist[u] < dist_null:
                    for v in graph[u]:
                        if pair_v[v] == -1:
                            dist_null = dist[u] + 1
                        elif dist[pair_v[v]] == float('inf'):
                            dist[pair_v[v]] = dist[u] + 1
                            queue.append(pair_v[v])
            return dist_null != float('inf')
        
        def dfs(u):
            for v in graph[u]:
                if pair_v[v] == -1 or (dist[pair_v[v]] == dist[u] + 1 and dfs(pair_v[v])):
                    pair_u[u] = v
                    pair_v[v] = u
                    return True
            dist[u] = float('inf')
            return False
        
        result = 0
        while bfs():
            for u in range(num_left):
                if pair_u[u] == -1:
                    if dfs(u):
                        result += 1
        return result
    
    while low <= high:
        mid = (low + high) // 2
        # Build adjacency list for current mid
        graph = []
        for u in range(N):
            adj = [v for v in range(M) if dist_sq[u][v] <= mid]
            graph.append(adj)
        current = hopcroft_karp(graph, N, M)
        if current >= K:
            answer = mid
            high = mid - 1
        else:
            low = mid + 1
    
    print(answer)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()<#sep#>-

import sys
from functools import lru_cache
from collections import deque

def main():
    n = int(sys.stdin.readline())
    grid = [sys.stdin.readline().strip() for _ in range(n)]
    
    visited = [[False]*n for _ in range(n)]
    components = []
    
    for i in range(n):
        for j in range(n):
            if grid[i][j] == '#' and not visited[i][j]:
                q = deque()
                q.append((i, j))
                visited[i][j] = True
                current_component = []
                min_row = max_row = i
                min_col = max_col = j
                current_component.append((i, j))
                while q:
                    x, y = q.popleft()
                    for dx, dy in [(-1,0), (1,0), (0,-1), (0,1)]:
                        nx, ny = x + dx, y + dy
                        if 0 <= nx < n and 0 <= ny < n and not visited[nx][ny] and grid[nx][ny] == '#':
                            visited[nx][ny] = True
                            q.append((nx, ny))
                            current_component.append((nx, ny))
                            min_row = min(min_row, nx)
                            max_row = max(max_row, nx)
                            min_col = min(min_col, ny)
                            max_col = max(max_col, ny)
                H = max_row - min_row + 1
                W = max_col - min_col + 1
                component_grid = [[0]*W for _ in range(H)]
                for (x, y) in current_component:
                    ci = x - min_row
                    cj = y - min_col
                    component_grid[ci][cj] = 1
                components.append((component_grid, H, W))
    
    total_cost = 0
    for (cg, H, W) in components:
        prefix = [[0]*(W+1) for _ in range(H+1)]
        for i in range(H):
            row_sum = 0
            for j in range(W):
                row_sum += cg[i][j]
                prefix[i+1][j+1] = prefix[i][j+1] + row_sum
        
        @lru_cache(maxsize=None)
        def dp(t, b, l, r):
            sum_ = prefix[b+1][r+1] - prefix[t][r+1] - prefix[b+1][l] + prefix[t][l]
            if sum_ == 0:
                return 0
            cost = max(b - t + 1, r - l + 1)
            min_split = float('inf')
            # Vertical splits
            for k in range(l, r):
                left = dp(t, b, l, k)
                right = dp(t, b, k+1, r)
                min_split = min(min_split, left + right)
            # Horizontal splits
            for k in range(t, b):
                top = dp(t, k, l, r)
                bottom = dp(k+1, b, l, r)
                min_split = min(min_split, top + bottom)
            return min(cost, min_split)
        
        total_cost += dp(0, H-1, 0, W-1)
    
    print(total_cost)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()<#sep#>-

import sys
from collections import deque

class Edge:
    def __init__(self, to, rev, capacity):
        self.to = to
        self.rev = rev
        self.capacity = capacity

class MaxFlow:
    def __init__(self, n):
        self.size = n
        self.graph = [[] for _ in range(n)]
    
    def add_edge(self, fr, to, cap):
        forward = Edge(to, len(self.graph[to]), cap)
        backward = Edge(fr, len(self.graph[fr]), 0)
        self.graph[fr].append(forward)
        self.graph[to].append(backward)
    
    def bfs_level(self, s, t, level):
        q = deque()
        level[:] = [-1] * self.size
        level[s] = 0
        q.append(s)
        while q:
            v = q.popleft()
            for edge in self.graph[v]:
                if edge.capacity > 0 and level[edge.to] == -1:
                    level[edge.to] = level[v] + 1
                    q.append(edge.to)
                    if edge.to == t:
                        return
    
    def dfs_flow(self, v, t, upTo, iter_, level):
        if v == t:
            return upTo
        for i in range(iter_[v], len(self.graph[v])):
            edge = self.graph[v][i]
            if edge.capacity > 0 and level[v] < level[edge.to]:
                d = self.dfs_flow(edge.to, t, min(upTo, edge.capacity), iter_, level)
                if d > 0:
                    edge.capacity -= d
                    self.graph[edge.to][edge.rev].capacity += d
                    return d
            iter_[v] += 1
        return 0
    
    def max_flow(self, s, t):
        flow = 0
        level = [-1] * self.size
        while True:
            self.bfs_level(s, t, level)
            if level[t] == -1:
                return flow
            iter_ = [0] * self.size
            while True:
                f = self.dfs_flow(s, t, float('inf'), iter_, level)
                if f == 0:
                    break
                flow += f

def main():
    h, w, W = map(int, sys.stdin.readline().split())
    B = []
    total = 0
    for _ in range(h):
        row = list(map(int, sys.stdin.readline().split()))
        B.append(row)
        total += sum(row)
    
    # Collect power supplies
    power_supplies = []
    for i in range(1, h):
        for j in range(1, w):
            if (i + j) % 2 == 0:
                power_supplies.append((i, j))
    
    # Assign IDs
    num_bulbs = h * w
    num_ps = len(power_supplies)
    bulb_start = 2
    ps_start = 2 + num_bulbs
    total_nodes = ps_start + num_ps
    mf = MaxFlow(total_nodes)
    
    # Add edges from source to bulbs
    for x in range(1, h+1):
        for y in range(1, w+1):
            bulb_id = bulb_start + (x-1)*w + (y-1)
            mf.add_edge(0, bulb_id, B[x-1][y-1])
    
    # Add edges from power supplies to sink and bulbs
    INF = 10**18
    for idx, (i, j) in enumerate(power_supplies):
        ps_id = ps_start + idx
        mf.add_edge(ps_id, 1, W)
        # Determine covered bulbs
        for dx in [0, 1]:
            for dy in [0, 1]:
                x = i + dx
                y = j + dy
                if 1 <= x <= h and 1 <= y <= w:
                    bulb_id = bulb_start + (x-1)*w + (y-1)
                    mf.add_edge(ps_id, bulb_id, INF)
    
    max_flow = mf.max_flow(0, 1)
    print(total - max_flow)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()<#sep#>-

n, m = map(int, input().split())
grid = [input().strip() for _ in range(n)]

# Precompute the max steps for each direction
max_north = [[0] * m for _ in range(n)]
for x in range(n):
    for y in range(m):
        if grid[x][y] == '#':
            max_north[x][y] = 0
        else:
            if x == 0:
                max_north[x][y] = 0
            else:
                if grid[x-1][y] == '#':
                    max_north[x][y] = 0
                else:
                    max_north[x][y] = 1 + max_north[x-1][y]

max_south = [[0] * m for _ in range(n)]
for x in range(n-1, -1, -1):
    for y in range(m):
        if grid[x][y] == '#':
            max_south[x][y] = 0
        else:
            if x == n-1:
                max_south[x][y] = 0
            else:
                if grid[x+1][y] == '#':
                    max_south[x][y] = 0
                else:
                    max_south[x][y] = 1 + max_south[x+1][y]

max_west = [[0] * m for _ in range(n)]
for y in range(m):
    for x in range(n):
        if grid[x][y] == '#':
            max_west[x][y] = 0
        else:
            if y == 0:
                max_west[x][y] = 0
            else:
                if grid[x][y-1] == '#':
                    max_west[x][y] = 0
                else:
                    max_west[x][y] = 1 + max_west[x][y-1]

max_east = [[0] * m for _ in range(n)]
for y in range(m-1, -1, -1):
    for x in range(n):
        if grid[x][y] == '#':
            max_east[x][y] = 0
        else:
            if y == m-1:
                max_east[x][y] = 0
            else:
                if grid[x][y+1] == '#':
                    max_east[x][y] = 0
                else:
                    max_east[x][y] = 1 + max_east[x][y+1]

# Collect starting cells (letters)
start_cells = []
for x in range(1, n-1):
    for y in range(1, m-1):
        c = grid[x][y]
        if c != '.' and c != '#':
            start_cells.append((x, y, c))

k = int(input())
instructions = [input().split() for _ in range(k)]
instructions = [(d, int(l)) for d, l in instructions]

valid_letters = []
for x, y, c in start_cells:
    current_x, current_y = x, y
    valid = True
    for dir, ln in instructions:
        if dir == 'N':
            required = ln
            available = max_north[current_x][current_y]
            if required > available:
                valid = False
                break
            current_x -= ln
        elif dir == 'S':
            required = ln
            available = max_south[current_x][current_y]
            if required > available:
                valid = False
                break
            current_x += ln
        elif dir == 'W':
            required = ln
            available = max_west[current_x][current_y]
            if required > available:
                valid = False
                break
            current_y -= ln
        elif dir == 'E':
            required = ln
            available = max_east[current_x][current_y]
            if required > available:
                valid = False
                break
            current_y += ln
    if valid:
        valid_letters.append(c)

valid_letters.sort()
if valid_letters:
    print(''.join(valid_letters))

import sys
from itertools import permutations

def are_non_overlapping(r1, c1, r2, c2):
    if (r1 + 2 < r2) or (r2 + 2 < r1):
        return True
    if (c1 + 2 < c2) or (c2 + 2 < c1):
        return True
    return False

def check_valid_squares(grid, n, m):
    valid_squares = []
    for r in range(n - 2):
        for c in range(m - 2):
            suits = set()
            ranks = set()
            for dr in range(3):
                for dc in range(3):
                    card = grid[r + dr][c + dc]
                    suits.add(card[1])
                    ranks.add(card[0])
            if len(suits) == 1 or len(ranks) == 9:
                valid_squares.append((r, c))
    for i in range(len(valid_squares)):
        for j in range(i + 1, len(valid_squares)):
            s1 = valid_squares[i]
            s2 = valid_squares[j]
            if are_non_overlapping(s1[0], s1[1], s2[0], s2[1]):
                return (s1, s2)
    return None

def main():
    n, m = map(int, sys.stdin.readline().split())
    grid = [sys.stdin.readline().split() for _ in range(n)]
    jokers = []
    used_cards = set()
    for i in range(n):
        for j in range(m):
            card = grid[i][j]
            if card in ('J1', 'J2'):
                jokers.append((i, j))
            used_cards.add(card)
    num_jokers = len(jokers)
    
    regular_ranks = ['2','3','4','5','6','7','8','9','T','J','Q','K','A']
    regular_suits = ['C','D','H','S']
    regular_cards = [r + s for s in regular_suits for r in regular_ranks]
    available = [card for card in regular_cards if card not in used_cards]
    
    solution_found = False
    output_replacement = None
    output_squares = None
    
    if num_jokers == 0:
        squares = check_valid_squares(grid, n, m)
        if squares:
            solution_found = True
            output_replacement = "There are no jokers."
            output_squares = squares
    elif num_jokers == 1:
        if available:
            j_pos = jokers[0]
            original_j = grid[j_pos[0]][j_pos[1]]
            for replacement in available:
                new_grid = [row.copy() for row in grid]
                new_grid[j_pos[0]][j_pos[1]] = replacement
                squares = check_valid_squares(new_grid, n, m)
                if squares:
                    solution_found = True
                    j_number = original_j[1]
                    output_replacement = f"Replace J{j_number} with {replacement}."
                    output_squares = squares
                    break
    else:
        if len(available) >= 2:
            j1_original = grid[jokers[0][0]][jokers[0][1]]
            j2_original = grid[jokers[1][0]][jokers[1][1]]
            for x, y in permutations(available, 2):
                new_grid = [row.copy() for row in grid]
                for (i, j) in jokers:
                    original = grid[i][j]
                    if original == 'J1':
                        new_grid[i][j] = x
                    else:
                        new_grid[i][j] = y
                squares = check_valid_squares(new_grid, n, m)
                if squares:
                    solution_found = True
                    output_replacement = f"Replace J1 with {x} and J2 with {y}."
                    output_squares = squares
                    break
    
    if solution_found:
        print("Solution exists.")
        print(output_replacement)
        s1, s2 = output_squares
        print(f"Put the first square to ({s1[0]+1}, {s1[1]+1}).")
        print(f"Put the second square to ({s2[0]+1}, {s2[1]+1}).")
    else:
        print("No solution.")

if __name__ == "__main__":

MOD = 10**9 + 7

n, k = map(int, input().split())
weights = list(map(int, input().split()))

A = weights.count(50)
B = weights.count(100)

# Check if any individual's weight exceeds the boat's capacity
if (A > 0 and 50 > k) or (B > 0 and 100 > k):
    print(-1)
    print(0)
    exit()

# Precompute combination numbers
comb = [[0] * 51 for _ in range(51)]
comb[0][0] = 1
for i in range(1, 51):
    comb[i][0] = 1
    for j in range(1, i + 1):
        comb[i][j] = (comb[i-1][j-1] + comb[i-1][j]) % MOD

# Initialize BFS
INF = float('inf')
max_a, max_b = A, B
distance = [[[INF] * 2 for _ in range(max_b + 1)] for __ in range(max_a + 1)]
ways = [[[0] * 2 for _ in range(max_b + 1)] for __ in range(max_a + 1)]
distance[A][B][0] = 0
ways[A][B][0] = 1

from collections import deque
q = deque()
q.append((A, B, 0))

processed = [[[False] * 2 for _ in range(max_b + 1)] for __ in range(max_a + 1)]
found = False

while q:
    a, b, pos = q.popleft()
    if processed[a][b][pos]:
        continue
    processed[a][b][pos] = True

    # Check if we've reached the goal state
    if a == 0 and b == 0 and pos == 1:
        print(distance[a][b][pos])
        print(ways[a][b][pos] % MOD)
        found = True
        break

    if pos == 0:
        # Move from left to right
        for x in range(a + 1):
            for y in range(b + 1):
                if x + y == 0:
                    continue
                if 50 * x + 100 * y > k:
                    continue
                new_a = a - x
                new_b = b - y
                new_pos = 1
                c_x = comb[a][x] if a >= x else 0
                c_y = comb[b][y] if b >= y else 0
                total_comb = (c_x * c_y) % MOD
                if total_comb == 0:
                    continue
                new_dist = distance[a][b][pos] + 1
                if new_dist < distance[new_a][new_b][new_pos]:
                    distance[new_a][new_b][new_pos] = new_dist
                    ways[new_a][new_b][new_pos] = (ways[a][b][pos] * total_comb) % MOD
                    q.append((new_a, new_b, new_pos))
                elif new_dist == distance[new_a][new_b][new_pos]:
                    ways[new_a][new_b][new_pos] = (ways[new_a][new_b][new_pos] + ways[a][b][pos] * total_comb) % MOD
                    q.append((new_a, new_b, new_pos))
    else:
        # Move from right to left
        a_right = max_a - a
        b_right = max_b - b
        for x in range(a_right + 1):
            for y in range(b_right + 1):
                if x + y == 0:
                    continue
                if 50 * x + 100 * y > k:
                    continue
                new_a_val = a + x
                new_b_val = b + y
                new_pos = 0
                c_x = comb[a_right][x] if a_right >= x else 0
                c_y = comb[b_right][y] if b_right >= y else 0
                total_comb = (c_x * c_y) % MOD
                if total_comb == 0:
                    continue
                new_dist = distance[a][b][pos] + 1
                if new_dist < distance[new_a_val][new_b_val][new_pos]:
                    distance[new_a_val][new_b_val][new_pos] = new_dist
                    ways[new_a_val][new_b_val][new_pos] = (ways[a][b][pos] * total_comb) % MOD
                    q.append((new_a_val, new_b_val, new_pos))
                elif new_dist == distance[new_a_val][new_b_val][new_pos]:
                    ways[new_a_val][new_b_val][new_pos] = (ways[new_a_val][new_b_val][new_pos] + ways[a][b][pos] * total_comb) % MOD
                    q.append((new_a_val, new_b_val, new_pos))
