import sys

def main():
    import sys
    from sys import stdin

    input = stdin.read().splitlines()
    N = int(input[0])
    cases = input[1:N+1]

    for case in cases:
        pieces_str = case.split()
        pieces_rotations = []
        for s in pieces_str:
            rotations = []
            # Generate all four rotations for each piece
            rotations.append((s[0], s[1], s[2], s[3]))  # 0 degrees
            rotations.append((s[3], s[0], s[1], s[2]))  # 90 degrees
            rotations.append((s[2], s[3], s[0], s[1]))  # 180 degrees
            rotations.append((s[1], s[2], s[3], s[0]))  # 270 degrees
            pieces_rotations.append(rotations)

        def backtrack(mask, row, col, current_edges, prev_rows_bottom):
            if row == 3:
                return 1
            total = 0
            for piece_idx in range(9):
                if mask & (1 << piece_idx):
                    continue
                for rotation in range(4):
                    top, right, bottom, left = pieces_rotations[piece_idx][rotation]
                    # Check top constraint
                    if row > 0:
                        if top != prev_rows_bottom[row-1][col]:
                            continue
                    # Check left constraint
                    if col > 0:
                        if left != current_edges[col-1][0]:
                            continue
                    new_mask = mask | (1 << piece_idx)
                    new_current_edges = current_edges.copy()
                    new_current_edges.append((right, bottom))
                    if col == 2:
                        new_prev = prev_rows_bottom.copy()
                        new_prev.append([e[1] for e in new_current_edges])
                        total += backtrack(new_mask, row + 1, 0, [], new_prev)
                    else:
                        total += backtrack(new_mask, row, col + 1, new_current_edges, prev_rows_bottom)
            return total

        count = backtrack(0, 0, 0, [], [])
        print(count)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()<#sep#>-

import sys
from itertools import product

def generate_rotations(cube):
    def roll(v): return (v[0], v[2], v[4], v[1], v[3], v[5])
    def turn(v): return (v[2], v[0], v[1], v[3], v[4], v[5])
    def sequence(v):
        for _ in range(2):
            for _ in range(3):
                v = roll(v)
                yield v
                for _ in range(3):
                    v = turn(v)
                    yield v
            v = roll(turn(roll(v)))

    start = (0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5)
    seen = set()
    rotations = []
    for perm in sequence(start):
        if perm not in seen:
            seen.add(perm)
            rotations.append(perm)
    
    rotated = []
    for perm in rotations:
        rotated_cube = [cube[perm[i]] for i in range(6)]
        if rotated_cube not in rotated:
            rotated.append(rotated_cube)
    return rotated

def main():
    input = sys.stdin.read().split()
    ptr = 0
    while True:
        if ptr >= len(input):
            break
        n = int(input[ptr])
        ptr +=1
        if n ==0:
            break
        cubes = []
        for _ in range(n):
            cube = input[ptr:ptr+6]
            ptr +=6
            cubes.append(cube)
        
        face_colors = [{} for _ in range(6)]
        for cube in cubes:
            rotations = generate_rotations(cube)
            per_face = [set() for _ in range(6)]
            for rot in rotations:
                for i in range(6):
                    per_face[i].add(rot[i])
            for i in range(6):
                for c in per_face[i]:
                    face_colors[i][c] = face_colors[i].get(c, 0) + 1
        
        total =0
        for i in range(6):
            if not face_colors[i]:
                total +=n
            else:
                max_count = max(face_colors[i].values())
                total += (n - max_count)
        print(total)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()<#sep#>-

def main():
    import sys
    input = sys.stdin.read().split()
    ptr = 0
    N = int(input[ptr])
    ptr += 1
    Q = int(input[ptr])
    ptr += 1
    a = list(map(int, input[ptr:ptr+N]))
    ptr += N
    queries = list(map(int, input[ptr:ptr+Q]))
    
    prev = {}
    next_ = {}
    for i in range(N):
        if i == 0:
            prev[a[i]] = None
        else:
            prev[a[i]] = a[i-1]
        if i == N-1:
            next_[a[i]] = None
        else:
            next_[a[i]] = a[i+1]
    
    head = a[0]
    tail = a[-1]
    
    for q in queries:
        current_prev = prev.get(q)
        current_next = next_.get(q)
        
        if current_next is not None:
            new_head = current_next
            prev[q] = tail
            next_[tail] = q
        else:
            new_head = q
            prev[q] = None
        
        if current_prev is not None:
            next_[q] = head
            prev[head] = q
            new_tail = current_prev
        else:
            next_[q] = None
            new_tail = q
        
        head = new_head
        tail = new_tail
    
    result = []
    current = head
    while current is not None:
        result.append(str(current))
        current = next_.get(current)
    print(' '.join(result))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()<#sep#>-

from functools import lru_cache

def compute_sum(X):
    if X < 0:
        return 0
    digits = list(map(int, str(X)))
    n = len(digits)
    
    @lru_cache(maxsize=None)
    def dfs(pos, tight, sum_mod):
        if pos == n:
            return sum_mod
        total = 0
        max_d = digits[pos] if tight else 9
        for d in range(0, max_d + 1):
            new_tight = tight and (d == max_d)
            if d % 2 == 0:
                contrib = (2 * d) % 10
            else:
                contrib = d % 10
            new_sum_mod = (sum_mod + contrib) % 10
            total += dfs(pos + 1, new_tight, new_sum_mod)
        return total
    
    return dfs(0, True, 0)

def main():
    import sys
    input = sys.stdin.read().split()
    T = int(input[0])
    idx = 1
    for _ in range(T):
        A = int(input[idx])
        B = int(input[idx+1])
        idx += 2
        result = compute_sum(B) - compute_sum(A - 1)
        print(result)

import sys
from sys import stdin
sys.setrecursionlimit(1 << 25)

def main():
    input = sys.stdin.read().split()
    ptr = 0
    t = int(input[ptr])
    ptr += 1
    for _ in range(t):
        h, g = map(int, input[ptr:ptr+2])
        ptr += 2
        n = (1 << h) - 1
        a = list(map(int, input[ptr:ptr + n]))
        ptr += n
        a = [0] + a  # 1-based index

        min_sub = [0] * (n + 2)
        for i in range(n, 0, -1):
            left = 2 * i
            right = 2 * i + 1
            if left > n:
                min_sub[i] = a[i]
            else:
                min_sub[i] = min(min_sub[left], min_sub[right])

        m = (1 << g) - 1
        x = [0] * (m + 1)
        parent = [0] * (n + 1)

        def find_min_geq(i, T):
            res = float('inf')
            stack = [(i, False)]
            while stack:
                node, visited = stack.pop()
                if node > n or a[node] < T:
                    continue
                if not visited:
                    stack.append((node, True))
                    stack.append((2 * node + 1, False))
                    stack.append((2 * node, False))
                else:
                    if a[node] < res:
                        res = a[node]
            return res

        def compute_x(i):
            if i > m:
                return 0
            left = 2 * i
            right = 2 * i + 1
            if left > m:
                x[i] = min_sub[i]
                return x[i]
            x_left = compute_x(left)
            x_right = compute_x(right)
            T = max(x_left, x_right)
            x_i = find_min_geq(i, T)
            x[i] = x_i
            return x_i

        compute_x(1)

        remove_order = []

        def mark_removal(i):
            if i > n:
                return
            left = 2 * i
            right = 2 * i + 1
            mark_removal(left)
            mark_removal(right)
            if i > m or a[i] != x[i]:
                remove_order.append(i)

        mark_removal(1)
        print(sum(x[1:m+1]))
        print(' '.join(map(str, remove_order)))

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()<#sep#>-

    # Compute term1 and term2 with modulo operations to prevent negatives

import heapq
import bisect

n, m, s, t = map(int, input().split())
d = list(map(int, input().split()))
magics = []
for _ in range(m):
    a, b = map(int, input().split())
    magics.append((a, b))

sorted_magics = sorted(magics, key=lambda x: x[0])
a_values = [x[0] for x in sorted_magics]

INF = float('inf')
distance = [INF] * (n + 1)
distance[s] = 0

heap = []
heapq.heappush(heap, (0, s))

while heap:
    current_cost, current_max = heapq.heappop(heap)
    
    if current_max >= t:
        print(current_cost)
        exit()
    
    if current_cost > distance[current_max]:
        continue
    
    idx = bisect.bisect_right(a_values, current_max)
    for magic in sorted_magics[:idx]:
        a_i, b_i = magic
        new_max = max(current_max, b_i)
        added = d[b_i - 1] if b_i > current_max else 0
        new_cost = current_cost + added
        
        if new_cost < distance[new_max]:
            distance[new_max] = new_cost
            heapq.heappush(heap, (new_cost, new_max))<#sep#>-

    else:
        num_terms = C - a + 1
        sum_part1 = (x - C) * num_terms + (a + C) * num_terms // 2
        count = sum_part1

import sys

def main():
    n, k = map(int, sys.stdin.readline().split())
    a = list(map(int, sys.stdin.readline().split()))
    
    sorted_a = sorted(a)
    a = [0] + a  # 1-based indexing
    sorted_a = [0] + sorted_a  # 1-based indexing
    
    INF = float('inf')
    dp = [[INF] * (k + 1) for _ in range(n + 1)]
    dp[0][0] = 0
    
    for i in range(1, n + 1):
        for j in range(0, k + 1):
            if j > 0 and a[i] == sorted_a[i]:
                dp[i][j] = dp[i-1][j-1]
            if i > 1:
                cost = (sorted_a[i] - sorted_a[i-1]) ** 2
                if dp[i-1][j] != INF:
                    dp[i][j] = min(dp[i][j], dp[i-1][j] + cost)
            if j > 0 and i > 1 and a[i] == sorted_a[i]:
                cost = (sorted_a[i] - sorted_a[i-1]) ** 2
                if dp[i-1][j-1] != INF:
                    dp[i][j] = min(dp[i][j], dp[i-1][j-1] + cost)
    
    print(dp[n][k])

import sys
from collections import deque

def main():
    n, m = map(int, sys.stdin.readline().split())
    adj = [[] for _ in range(n + 1)]
    degrees = [0] * (n + 1)
    for _ in range(m):
        u, v = map(int, sys.stdin.readline().split())
        adj[u].append(v)
        adj[v].append(u)
        degrees[u] += 1
        degrees[v] += 1
    
    S = [i for i in range(1, n+1) if degrees[i] > 0]
    color = [-1] * (n + 1)
    possible = True
    
    for u in S:
        if color[u] == -1:
            queue = deque()
            queue.append(u)
            color[u] = 0
            while queue and possible:
                current = queue.popleft()
                for v in adj[current]:
                    if degrees[v] == 0:
                        continue
                    if color[v] == -1:
                        color[v] = color[current] ^ 1
                        queue.append(v)
                    elif color[v] == color[current]:
                        possible = False
                        break
                if not possible:
                    break
        if not possible:
            break
    
    if not possible:
        print(-1)
    else:
        A = [u for u in S if color[u] == 0]
        B = [u for u in S if color[u] == 1]
        print(len(A))
        print(' '.join(map(str, A)))
        print(len(B))
        print(' '.join(map(str, B)))

if __name__ == "__main__":

        c_i = max_possible

            c_i = max_possible - 1

            exit()
    
    # Check validity of c_i
    if c_i < 0 or c_i % 2 != desired_par or c_i > max_possible:
        print("NO")
        exit()

import sys
from collections import deque

def main():
    input = sys.stdin.read().split()
    ptr = 0
    T = int(input[ptr])
    ptr +=1
    for _ in range(T):
        N, M = int(input[ptr]), int(input[ptr+1])
        ptr +=2
        adj = [[] for _ in range(N+1)]
        degrees = [0]*(N+1)
        for __ in range(M):
            x = int(input[ptr])
            y = int(input[ptr+1])
            ptr +=2
            adj[x].append(y)
            adj[y].append(x)
            degrees[x] +=1
            degrees[y] +=1
        # Check if any degree exceeds 2
        valid = True
        for i in range(1, N+1):
            if degrees[i] > 2:
                valid = False
                break
        if not valid:
            print("NO")
            continue
        # Check each connected component
        visited = [False]*(N+1)
        possible = True
        for node in range(1, N+1):
            if not visited[node]:
                queue = deque()
                queue.append(node)
                visited[node] = True
                sum_deg = degrees[node]
                size = 1
                while queue:
                    current = queue.popleft()
                    for neighbor in adj[current]:
                        if not visited[neighbor]:
                            visited[neighbor] = True
                            sum_deg += degrees[neighbor]
                            size +=1
                            queue.append(neighbor)
                if sum_deg != 2 * (size -1):
                    possible = False
                    break
        print("YES" if possible else "NO")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()<#sep#>-

def main():
    import sys
    input = sys.stdin.read().split()
    idx = 0
    T = int(input[idx])
    idx += 1
    for _ in range(T):
        S = input[idx].strip()
        idx += 1
        N = int(input[idx])
        idx += 1
        words = [input[idx + i].strip() for i in range(N)]
        idx += N
        word_set = set(words)
        n = len(S)
        
        valid = [[False]*(n+1) for _ in range(n+1)]
        for a in range(n):
            for b in range(a+1, n+1):
                substr = S[a:b]
                if substr in word_set:
                    valid[a][b] = True
        
        grundy = [[0]*(n+1) for _ in range(n+1)]
        for l in range(1, n+1):
            for i in range(n - l +1):
                j = i + l
                mex_set = set()
                for a in range(i, j):
                    for b in range(a+1, j+1):
                        if valid[a][b]:
                            mex_set.add(grundy[i][a] ^ grundy[b][j])
                mex = 0
                while mex in mex_set:
                    mex += 1
                grundy[i][j] = mex
        
        if grundy[0][n] != 0:
            print("Teddy")
        else:
            print("Tracy")

a, b, c = map(int, input().split())
x1, y1, x2, y2 = map(int, input().split())

manhattan = abs(x1 - x2) + abs(y1 - y2)
min_dist = manhattan

cases = [
    (True, True),
    (True, False),
    (False, True),
    (False, False)
]

for a_horiz, b_horiz in cases:
    if a_horiz and a == 0:
        continue
    if not a_horiz and b == 0:
        continue
    if b_horiz and a == 0:
        continue
    if not b_horiz and b == 0:
        continue

    # Calculate point P for A
    if a_horiz:
        px = (-b * y1 - c) / a
        py = y1
    else:
        py = (-a * x1 - c) / b
        px = x1

    # Calculate point Q for B
    if b_horiz:
        qx = (-b * y2 - c) / a
        qy = y2
    else:
        qy = (-a * x2 - c) / b
        qx = x2

    d_A = abs(x1 - px) + abs(y1 - py)
    d_B = abs(x2 - qx) + abs(y2 - qy)
    euclidean = ((px - qx)**2 + (py - qy)**2)**0.5
    total = d_A + d_B + euclidean

    if total < min_dist:
        min_dist = total

print("{0:.10f}".format(min_dist))<#sep#>-

import sys
from sys import stdin
from collections import defaultdict, deque

sys.setrecursionlimit(1 << 25)

def main():
    input = sys.stdin.read().split()
    ptr = 0
    while True:
        n, m = map(int, input[ptr:ptr+2])
        ptr += 2
        if n == 0 and m == 0:
            break
        
        adj = [[] for _ in range(n+1)]
        paths = []
        for _ in range(m):
            s = int(input[ptr])
            path = list(map(int, input[ptr+1:ptr+1+s]))
            ptr += 1 + s
            for i in range(s-1):
                u = path[i]
                v = path[i+1]
                adj[u].append(v)
                adj[v].append(u)
        
        if m == 0:
            print(0)
            continue
        
        disc = [0] * (n+1)
        low = [0] * (n+1)
        time = 1
        bridges = []
        visited = [False] * (n+1)
        parent = [-1] * (n+1)
        
        def tarjan(u):
            nonlocal time
            disc[u] = low[u] = time
            time += 1
            visited[u] = True
            for v in adj[u]:
                if not visited[v]:
                    parent[v] = u
                    tarjan(v)
                    low[u] = min(low[u], low[v])
                    if low[v] > disc[u]:
                        bridges.append((u, v))
                        bridges.append((v, u))
                elif v != parent[u]:
                    low[u] = min(low[u], disc[v])
        
        for u in range(1, n+1):
            if not visited[u]:
                tarjan(u)
        
        bridge_set = set(bridges)
        
        parent_dsu = list(range(n+1))
        def find(u):
            if parent_dsu[u] != u:
                parent_dsu[u] = find(parent_dsu[u])
            return parent_dsu[u]
        
        def union(u, v):
            u_root = find(u)
            v_root = find(v)
            if u_root != v_root:
                parent_dsu[v_root] = u_root
        
        for u in range(1, n+1):
            for v in adj[u]:
                if u < v and (u, v) not in bridge_set:
                    union(u, v)
        
        components = defaultdict(list)
        for node in range(1, n+1):
            components[find(node)].append(node)
        
        bridge_tree = defaultdict(list)
        for u, v in bridges:
            if u > v:
                continue
            cu = find(u)
            cv = find(v)
            if cu != cv:
                bridge_tree[cu].append(cv)
                bridge_tree[cv].append(cu)
        
        leaves = [comp for comp in bridge_tree if len(bridge_tree[comp]) == 1]
        if not bridge_tree and n >= 1:
            leaves = []
        
        leaf_reps = []
        for comp in leaves:
            partner = bridge_tree[comp][0]
            found = False
            art_point = None
            for u in components[comp]:
                for v in adj[u]:
                    if (u, v) in bridge_set:
                        if find(u) == comp and find(v) == partner:
                            art_point = u
                            found = True
                            break
                        elif find(v) == comp and find(u) == partner:
                            art_point = v
                            found = True
                            break
                if found:
                    break
            
            if not found:
                art_point = comp
            
            nodes = components[comp]
            if len(nodes) > 1:
                for node in nodes:
                    if node != art_point:
                        rep = node
                        break
            else:
                rep = art_point
            leaf_reps.append(rep)
        
        A = (len(leaf_reps) + 1) // 2
        print(A)
        added = set()
        for i in range(0, len(leaf_reps), 2):
            if i+1 >= len(leaf_reps):
                u = leaf_reps[i]
                v = leaf_reps[0]
                if u > v:
                    u, v = v, u
                if u != v and (u, v) not in added:
                    print(u, v)
                    added.add((u, v))
            else:
                u = leaf_reps[i]
                v = leaf_reps[i+1]
                if u > v:
                    u, v = v, u
                if u != v and (u, v) not in added:
                    print(u, v)
                    added.add((u, v))

def generate_primes(m):
    primes = []
    candidate = 2
    while len(primes) < m:
        is_prime = True
        for p in primes:
            if p * p > candidate:
                break
            if candidate % p == 0:
                is_prime = False
                break
        if is_prime:
            primes.append(candidate)
        candidate += 1
    return primes

def get_factor_sequences(n):
    def dfs(m, max_factor):
        if m == 1:
            return [[]]
        sequences = []
        divisors = []
        for d in range(min(m, max_factor), 1, -1):
            if m % d == 0:
                divisors.append(d)
        for d in divisors:
            for seq in dfs(m // d, d):
                sequences.append([d] + seq)
        return sequences
    return dfs(n, n)

n = int(input())
if n == 1:
    print(1)
else:
    sequences = get_factor_sequences(n)
    if not sequences:
        print(1)
    else:
        max_prime_length = max(len(seq) for seq in sequences)
        primes = generate_primes(max_prime_length)
        min_num = float('inf')
        for seq in sequences:
            k = len(seq)
            primes_needed = primes[:k]
            current = 1
            valid = True
            for i in range(k):

def merge_intervals(intervals):
    if not intervals:
        return []
    sorted_intervals = sorted(intervals, key=lambda x: x[0])
    merged = [sorted_intervals[0]]
    for current in sorted_intervals[1:]:
        last = merged[-1]
        if current[0] < last[1] and last[0] < current[1]:
            new_start = min(last[0], current[0])
            new_end = max(last[1], current[1])
            merged[-1] = (new_start, new_end)
        else:
            merged.append(current)
    return merged

merged = merge_intervals(others)

current = alex_l
total = 0

for x, y in merged:
    if current >= alex_r:
        break
    if y <= current:
        continue
    if x > current:
        add_end = min(x, alex_r)
        if add_end > current:
            total += add_end - current
        current = max(y, current)
        current = min(current, alex_r)
    else:
        if y > current:
            current = min(y, alex_r)

if current < alex_r:
    total += alex_r - current

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

struct StateInfo {
    int min_step;
    string path;
};

struct State {
    int step;
    string path;
    int i, j;
    int mask;

    State(int s, const string& p, int i_, int j_, int m) : step(s), path(p), i(i_), j(j_), mask(m) {}
};

struct CompareState {
    bool operator()(const State& a, const State& b) {
        if (a.step != b.step) return a.step > b.step;
        if (a.path != b.path) return a.path > b.path;
        if (a.i != b.i) return a.i > b.i;
        if (a.j != b.j) return a.j > b.j;
        return a.mask > b.mask;
    }
};

int main() {
    int n, m, k;
    cin >> n >> m >> k;

    vector<string> grid(n);
    int start_i = -1, start_j = -1;
    int end_i = -1, end_j = -1;

    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        cin >> grid[i];
        for (int j = 0; j < m; ++j) {
            if (grid[i][j] == 'S') {
                start_i = i;
                start_j = j;
            } else if (grid[i][j] == 'T') {
                end_i = i;
                end_j = j;
            }
        }
    }

    vector<pair<int, int>> dirs = {{-1, 0}, {1, 0}, {0, -1}, {0, 1}};
    vector<vector<map<int, StateInfo>>> visited(n, vector<map<int, StateInfo>>(m));
    priority_queue<State, vector<State>, CompareState> pq;

    pq.emplace(0, "", start_i, start_j, 0);
    visited[start_i][start_j][0] = {0, ""};

    string result = "-1";
    bool found = false;

    while (!pq.empty()) {
        State current = pq.top();
        pq.pop();

        int i = current.i, j = current.j, mask = current.mask;
        string path = current.path;
        int step = current.step;

        auto& cell_visited = visited[i][j];
        auto it = cell_visited.find(mask);
        if (it == cell_visited.end()) continue;
        StateInfo& info = it->second;
        if (info.min_step != step || info.path != path) continue;

        if (i == end_i && j == end_j) {
            result = path;
            found = true;
            break;
        }

        for (auto& dir : dirs) {
            int ni = i + dir.first, nj = j + dir.second;
            if (ni < 0 || ni >= n || nj < 0 || nj >= m) continue;

            char c = grid[ni][nj];
            if (c == 'S') continue;

            if (c == 'T') {
                if (ni == end_i && nj == end_j) {
                    result = path;
                    found = true;
                    break;
                }
                continue;
            }

            int new_mask = mask;
            int bit = c - 'a';
            new_mask |= (1 << bit);
            if (__builtin_popcount(new_mask) > k) continue;

            string new_path = path + c;
            int new_step = step + 1;

            auto& new_cell = visited[ni][nj];
            auto new_it = new_cell.find(new_mask);
            if (new_it == new_cell.end()) {
                new_cell[new_mask] = {new_step, new_path};
                pq.emplace(new_step, new_path, ni, nj, new_mask);
            } else {
                StateInfo& new_info = new_it->second;
                if (new_info.min_step == new_step && new_path < new_info.path) {
                    new_info.path = new_path;
                    pq.emplace(new_step, new_path, ni, nj, new_mask);
                }
            }
        }
        if (found) break;
    }

    cout << (found ? result : "-1") << endl;
    return 0;
}<#sep#>-

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

const int MAXN = 500005;
int n, m;
vector<pair<int, int>> adj[MAXN]; // to, type
int parent[MAXN];
vector<int> edge_children; // for each edge, the child node

struct Edge {
    int u, v, t;
} edges[MAXN];

int parity[MAXN], depth[MAXN];
int in[MAXN], out[MAXN], timer = 0;

void dfs(int u, int p) {
    in[u] = ++timer;
    for (auto &e : adj[u]) {
        int v = e.first, t = e.second;
        if (v == p) continue;
        parent[v] = u;
        parity[v] = parity[u] ^ t;
        depth[v] = depth[u] + 1;
        dfs(v, u);
    }
    out[u] = timer;
}

struct SegmentTree {
    struct Node {
        int max0, sec0;
        int max1, sec1;
        bool flip;
        Node() : max0(-1), sec0(-1), max1(-1), sec1(-1), flip(false) {}
    } tree[4 * MAXN];

    void push(int v, int l, int r) {
        if (tree[v].flip) {
            swap(tree[v].max0, tree[v].max1);
            swap(tree[v].sec0, tree[v].sec1);
            if (l != r) {
                tree[2*v].flip ^= 1;
                tree[2*v+1].flip ^= 1;
            }
            tree[v].flip = false;
        }
    }

    void update(int v, int l, int r, int ul, int ur) {
        push(v, l, r);
        if (ur < l || r < ul) return;
        if (ul <= l && r <= ur) {
            tree[v].flip ^= 1;
            push(v, l, r);
            return;
        }
        int mid = (l + r) / 2;
        update(2*v, l, mid, ul, ur);
        update(2*v+1, mid+1, r, ul, ur);
        vector<int> list0 = {
            tree[2*v].max0, tree[2*v].sec0,
            tree[2*v+1].max0, tree[2*v+1].sec0
        };
        sort(list0.rbegin(), list0.rend());
        tree[v].max0 = list0[0];
        tree[v].sec0 = list0[1];
        vector<int> list1 = {
            tree[2*v].max1, tree[2*v].sec1,
            tree[2*v+1].max1, tree[2*v+1].sec1
        };
        sort(list1.rbegin(), list1.rend());
        tree[v].max1 = list1[0];
        tree[v].sec1 = list1[1];
    }

    pair<int, int> getMax(int p) {
        push(1, 1, n);
        if (p == 0) {
            return {tree[1].max0, tree[1].sec0};
        } else {
            return {tree[1].max1, tree[1].sec1};
        }
    }

    void build(int v, int l, int r) {
        if (l == r) {
            tree[v].max0 = tree[v].max1 = tree[v].sec0 = tree[v].sec1 = -1;
            // Actual value will be set during the initial setup
            return;
        }
        int mid = (l + r) / 2;
        build(2*v, l, mid);
        build(2*v+1, mid+1, r);
    }

    void set(int v, int l, int r, int pos, int p, int d) {
        push(v, l, r);
        if (l == r) {
            if (p == 0) {
                tree[v].max0 = d;
                tree[v].sec0 = -1;
                tree[v].max1 = -1;
                tree[v].sec1 = -1;
            } else {
                tree[v].max1 = d;
                tree[v].sec1 = -1;
                tree[v].max0 = -1;
                tree[v].sec0 = -1;
            }
            return;
        }
        int mid = (l + r) / 2;
        if (pos <= mid) {
            set(2*v, l, mid, pos, p, d);
        } else {
            set(2*v+1, mid+1, r, pos, p, d);
        }
        push(2*v, l, mid);
        push(2*v+1, mid+1, r);
        vector<int> list0 = {
            tree[2*v].max0, tree[2*v].sec0,
            tree[2*v+1].max0, tree[2*v+1].sec0
        };
        sort(list0.rbegin(), list0.rend());
        tree[v].max0 = list0[0];
        tree[v].sec0 = list0[1];
        vector<int> list1 = {
            tree[2*v].max1, tree[2*v].sec1,
            tree[2*v+1].max1, tree[2*v+1].sec1
        };
        sort(list1.rbegin(), list1.rend());
        tree[v].max1 = list1[0];
        tree[v].sec1 = list1[1];
    }
} st;

int main() {
    ios_base::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(nullptr);

    cin >> n;
    for (int i = 1; i <= n-1; i++) {
        int u, v, t;
        cin >> u >> v >> t;
        edges[i] = {u, v, t};
        adj[u].push_back({v, t});
        adj[v].push_back({u, t});
    }

    dfs(1, 0);

    // Determine the child for each edge
    edge_children.resize(n);
    for (int i = 1; i <= n-1; i++) {
        int u = edges[i].u, v = edges[i].v;
        if (parent[v] == u) {
            edge_children[i] = v;
        } else if (parent[u] == v) {
            edge_children[i] = u;
        } else {
            // This should not happen as the tree is valid
            assert(false);
        }
    }

    st.build(1, 1, n);
    for (int i = 1; i <= n; i++) {
        st.set(1, 1, n, in[i], parity[i], depth[i]);
    }

    cin >> m;
    while (m--) {
        int id;
        cin >> id;
        int v = edge_children[id];
        st.update(1, 1, n, in[v], out[v]);

        auto [max0, sec0] = st.getMax(0);
        auto [max1, sec1] = st.getMax(1);

        int ans = 0;
        if (max0 != -1 && sec0 != -1) ans = max(ans, max0 + sec0);
        if (max1 != -1 && sec1 != -1) ans = max(ans, max1 + sec1);
        cout << ans << '\n';
    }

    return 0;
}<#sep#>-

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

struct MergedInterval {
    int min_li, max_li, ri;
};

struct SegmentTreeNode {
    vector<pair<int, int>> sorted_max_li;
    vector<int> suffix_max;
};

vector<MergedInterval> merged;
vector<SegmentTreeNode> seg_tree;

void build(int node, int l, int r) {
    if (l == r) {
        seg_tree[node].sorted_max_li = {{merged[l].max_li, merged[l].ri}};
        seg_tree[node].suffix_max = {merged[l].ri};
        return;
    }
    int mid = (l + r) / 2;
    build(2 * node + 1, l, mid);
    build(2 * node + 2, mid + 1, r);
    auto& left = seg_tree[2 * node + 1].sorted_max_li;
    auto& right = seg_tree[2 * node + 2].sorted_max_li;
    seg_tree[node].sorted_max_li.resize(left.size() + right.size());
    merge(left.begin(), left.end(), right.begin(), right.end(), seg_tree[node].sorted_max_li.begin(),
          [](const pair<int, int>& a, const pair<int, int>& b) {
              return a.first < b.first;
          });
    int max_ri = -1;
    seg_tree[node].suffix_max.resize(seg_tree[node].sorted_max_li.size());
    for (int i = seg_tree[node].sorted_max_li.size() - 1; i >= 0; --i) {
        max_ri = max(max_ri, seg_tree[node].sorted_max_li[i].second);
        seg_tree[node].suffix_max[i] = max_ri;
    }
}

int query_max(int node, int node_l, int node_r, int q_l, int q_r, int x) {
    if (node_r < q_l || node_l > q_r) return -1;
    if (q_l <= node_l && node_r <= q_r) {
        auto& vec = seg_tree[node].sorted_max_li;
        auto it = lower_bound(vec.begin(), vec.end(), make_pair(x, 0));
        if (it == vec.end()) return -1;
        return seg_tree[node].suffix_max[it - vec.begin()];
    }
    int mid = (node_l + node_r) / 2;
    return max(query_max(2 * node + 1, node_l, mid, q_l, q_r, x),
              query_max(2 * node + 2, mid + 1, node_r, q_l, q_r, x));
}

int main() {
    ios::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(nullptr);

    int n, m;
    cin >> n >> m;
    vector<pair<int, int>> ropes(m);
    for (auto& [l, r] : ropes) cin >> l >> r;
    sort(ropes.begin(), ropes.end(), [](const auto& a, const auto& b) { return a.second < b.second; });

    for (const auto& [l, r] : ropes) {
        if (!merged.empty() && l <= merged.back().ri) {
            auto& back = merged.back();
            back.min_li = min(back.min_li, l);
            back.max_li = max(back.max_li, l);
            back.ri = r;
        } else {
            merged.push_back({l, l, r});
        }
    }

    int merged_size = merged.size();
    seg_tree.resize(4 * merged_size);
    if (merged_size > 0) build(0, 0, merged_size - 1);

    int q;
    cin >> q;
    while (q--) {
        int x, y;
        cin >> x >> y;
        int k = -1;
        if (!merged.empty()) {
            auto it = upper_bound(merged.begin(), merged.end(), y,
                                  [](int val, const MergedInterval& mi) { return val < mi.ri; });
            if (it != merged.begin()) k = prev(it) - merged.begin();
        }
        if (k == -1) {
            cout << x << '\n';
            continue;
        }
        int res = query_max(0, 0, merged_size - 1, 0, k, x);
        cout << (res != -1 ? res : x) << '\n';
    }
}<#sep#>-

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

const long long INF = 1e18;
const long long NINF = -1e18;

struct Edge {
    int to, rev;
    long long cap;
    Edge(int _to, int _rev, long long _cap) : to(_to), rev(_rev), cap(_cap) {}
};

class Dinic {
public:
    int n;
    vector<vector<Edge>> g;
    vector<int> level, ptr;

    Dinic(int _n) : n(_n), g(_n), level(_n), ptr(_n) {}

    void add_edge(int u, int v, long long cap) {
        g[u].emplace_back(v, g[v].size(), cap);
        g[v].emplace_back(u, g[u].size()-1, 0);
    }

    bool bfs(int s, int t) {
        fill(level.begin(), level.end(), -1);
        queue<int> q;
        level[s] = 0;
        q.push(s);
        while (!q.empty()) {
            int u = q.front();
            q.pop();
            for (const Edge& e : g[u]) {
                if (e.cap > 0 && level[e.to] == -1) {
                    level[e.to] = level[u] + 1;
                    q.push(e.to);
                    if (e.to == t) return true;
                }
            }
        }
        return false;
    }

    long long dfs(int u, int t, long long flow) {
        if (u == t) return flow;
        for (int& i = ptr[u]; i < g[u].size(); ++i) {
            Edge& e = g[u][i];
            if (e.cap > 0 && level[e.to] == level[u] + 1) {
                long long pushed = dfs(e.to, t, min(flow, e.cap));
                if (pushed > 0) {
                    e.cap -= pushed;
                    g[e.to][e.rev].cap += pushed;
                    return pushed;
                }
            }
        }
        return 0;
    }

    long long max_flow(int s, int t) {
        long long flow = 0;
        while (bfs(s, t)) {
            fill(ptr.begin(), ptr.end(), 0);
            while (long long pushed = dfs(s, t, INF)) {
                flow += pushed;
            }
        }
        return flow;
    }
};

struct Base {
    int d, g;
    Base(int d, int g) : d(d), g(g) {}
    bool operator<(const Base& other) const {
        return d < other.d;
    }
};

int main() {
    ios::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(0);

    int n, m;
    cin >> n >> m;
    vector<vector<int>> adj(n+1);
    for (int i = 0; i < m; ++i) {
        int u, v;
        cin >> u >> v;
        adj[u].push_back(v);
        adj[v].push_back(u);
    }

    vector<vector<int>> dist(n+1, vector<int>(n+1, -1));
    for (int u = 1; u <= n; ++u) {
        queue<int> q;
        q.push(u);
        dist[u][u] = 0;
        while (!q.empty()) {
            int curr = q.front();
            q.pop();
            for (int v : adj[curr]) {
                if (dist[u][v] == -1) {
                    dist[u][v] = dist[u][curr] + 1;
                    q.push(v);
                }
            }
        }
    }

    int s, b, k;
    cin >> s >> b >> k;

    vector<vector<Base>> bases_grouped(n+1);
    for (int i = 0; i < b; ++i) {
        int x, d, g;
        cin >> x >> d >> g;
        bases_grouped[x].emplace_back(d, g);
    }

    vector<vector<Base>> processed_bases(n+1);
    for (int x = 1; x <= n; ++x) {
        if (bases_grouped[x].empty()) continue;
        sort(bases_grouped[x].begin(), bases_grouped[x].end());
        int max_g = 0;
        vector<Base> sorted_bases;
        for (auto& base : bases_grouped[x]) {
            max_g = max(max_g, base.g);
            sorted_bases.emplace_back(base.d, max_g);
        }
        processed_bases[x] = move(sorted_bases);
    }

    vector<long long> profit(s+1);
    long long sum_positive = 0;
    for (int i = 1; i <= s; ++i) {
        int x, a, f, p;
        cin >> x >> a >> f >> p;

        long long max_g = -1;
        for (int x_b = 1; x_b <= n; ++x_b) {
            if (dist[x][x_b] == -1 || dist[x][x_b] > f) continue;
            const vector<Base>& bases = processed_bases[x_b];
            if (bases.empty()) continue;

            int left = 0, right = (int)bases.size() - 1;
            int best = -1;
            while (left <= right) {
                int mid = (left + right) / 2;
                if (bases[mid].d <= a) {
                    best = mid;
                    left = mid + 1;
                } else {
                    right = mid - 1;
                }
            }
            if (best == -1) continue;
            long long current_g = bases[best].g;
            if (current_g > max_g) max_g = current_g;
        }

        if (max_g == -1) profit[i] = NINF;
        else profit[i] = max_g - p;

        if (profit[i] > 0) sum_positive += profit[i];
    }

    vector<pair<int, int>> dependencies;
    for (int i = 0; i < k; ++i) {
        int s1, s2;
        cin >> s1 >> s2;
        dependencies.emplace_back(s1, s2);
    }

    int source = 0, sink = s + 1;
    Dinic dinic(sink + 1);

    for (int i = 1; i <= s; ++i) {
        if (profit[i] > 0) dinic.add_edge(source, i, profit[i]);
        else dinic.add_edge(i, sink, -profit[i]);
    }

    for (auto& dep : dependencies) {
        dinic.add_edge(dep.first, dep.second, INF);
    }

    long long max_flow = dinic.max_flow(source, sink);
    cout << max(sum_positive - max_flow, 0LL) << endl;

    return 0;
}<#sep#>-

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

struct Node {
    int l, r;
    int min_val, max_val;
    int sub;
    Node *left, *right;
    Node(int l_, int r_) : l(l_), r(r_), min_val(0), max_val(0), sub(0), left(nullptr), right(nullptr) {}
};

Node* build(const vector<int>& a, int l, int r) {
    Node* node = new Node(l, r);
    if (l == r) {
        node->min_val = node->max_val = a[l];
        return node;
    }
    int mid = (l + r) / 2;
    node->left = build(a, l, mid);
    node->right = build(a, mid + 1, r);
    node->min_val = min(node->left->min_val, node->right->min_val);
    node->max_val = max(node->left->max_val, node->right->max_val);
    return node;
}

void push(Node* node) {
    if (node->sub == 0 || node->left == nullptr) return;
    node->left->sub += node->sub;
    node->left->min_val -= node->sub;
    node->left->max_val -= node->sub;
    node->right->sub += node->sub;
    node->right->min_val -= node->sub;
    node->right->max_val -= node->sub;
    node->sub = 0;
}

void update_range(Node* node, int l, int r, int x) {
    if (node->r < l || node->l > r) return;
    push(node);
    if (node->left) {
        node->min_val = min(node->left->min_val, node->right->min_val);
        node->max_val = max(node->left->max_val, node->right->max_val);
    }
    if (l <= node->l && node->r <= r) {
        if (node->max_val <= x) return;
        if (node->min_val > x) {
            node->sub += x;
            node->min_val -= x;
            node->max_val -= x;
            return;
        }
    }
    update_range(node->left, l, r, x);
    update_range(node->right, l, r, x);
    node->min_val = min(node->left->min_val, node->right->min_val);
    node->max_val = max(node->left->max_val, node->right->max_val);
}

int query_count(Node* node, int l, int r, int x) {
    if (node->r < l || node->l > r) return 0;
    push(node);
    if (node->left) {
        node->min_val = min(node->left->min_val, node->right->min_val);
        node->max_val = max(node->left->max_val, node->right->max_val);
    }
    if (x < node->min_val || x > node->max_val) return 0;
    if (l <= node->l && node->r <= r) {
        if (node->min_val == node->max_val) {
            return (node->min_val == x) ? (node->r - node->l + 1) : 0;
        }
    }
    return query_count(node->left, l, r, x) + query_count(node->right, l, r, x);
}

int main() {
    ios::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(nullptr);
    int n, m;
    cin >> n >> m;
    vector<int> a(n);
    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        cin >> a[i];
    }
    Node* root = build(a, 0, n - 1);
    while (m--) {
        int op, l, r, x;
        cin >> op >> l >> r >> x;
        --l; --r;
        if (op == 1) {
            update_range(root, l, r, x);
        } else {
            cout << query_count(root, l, r, x) << '\n';
        }
    }
    return 0;
}<#sep#>-

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

struct SegmentTree {
    vector<vector<int>> tree;
    int n;

    SegmentTree(const vector<int>& p) {
        n = p.size();
        tree.resize(4 * n);
        build(0, 0, n - 1, p);
    }

    void build(int node, int l, int r, const vector<int>& p) {
        if (l == r) {
            tree[node] = {p[l]};
            return;
        }
        int mid = (l + r) / 2;
        build(2 * node + 1, l, mid, p);
        build(2 * node + 2, mid + 1, r, p);
        merge(tree[2*node+1].begin(), tree[2*node+1].end(),
              tree[2*node+2].begin(), tree[2*node+2].end(),
              back_inserter(tree[node]));
    }

    int query_gt(int a, int b, int x) {
        return query_gt(0, 0, n - 1, a, b, x);
    }

    int query_lt(int a, int b, int x) {
        return query_lt(0, 0, n - 1, a, b, x);
    }

    int query_between(int a, int b, int low, int high) {
        return query_between(0, 0, n - 1, a, b, low, high);
    }

private:
    int query_gt(int node, int l, int r, int a, int b, int x) {
        if (a > b || r < a || l > b) return 0;
        if (a <= l && r <= b) {
            auto it = upper_bound(tree[node].begin(), tree[node].end(), x);
            return tree[node].end() - it;
        }
        int mid = (l + r) / 2;
        return query_gt(2 * node + 1, l, mid, a, b, x) +
               query_gt(2 * node + 2, mid + 1, r, a, b, x);
    }

    int query_lt(int node, int l, int r, int a, int b, int x) {
        if (a > b || r < a || l > b) return 0;
        if (a <= l && r <= b) {
            auto it = lower_bound(tree[node].begin(), tree[node].end(), x);
            return it - tree[node].begin();
        }
        int mid = (l + r) / 2;
        return query_lt(2 * node + 1, l, mid, a, b, x) +
               query_lt(2 * node + 2, mid + 1, r, a, b, x);
    }

    int query_between(int node, int l, int r, int a, int b, int low, int high) {
        if (a > b || r < a || l > b) return 0;
        if (a <= l && r <= b) {
            auto it_low = upper_bound(tree[node].begin(), tree[node].end(), low);
            auto it_high = lower_bound(tree[node].begin(), tree[node].end(), high);
            return it_high - it_low;
        }
        int mid = (l + r) / 2;
        return query_between(2 * node + 1, l, mid, a, b, low, high) +
               query_between(2 * node + 2, mid + 1, r, a, b, low, high);
    }
};

int main() {
    ios_base::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(nullptr);

    int n, q;
    cin >> n >> q;
    vector<int> p(n);
    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        cin >> p[i];
    }

    SegmentTree st(p);

    auto C2 = [](long long x) {
        return x * (x - 1) / 2;
    };

    while (q--) {
        int l, d, r, u;
        cin >> l >> d >> r >> u;
        l--; r--; // Convert to 0-based columns

        long long group1 = C2(l) + C2(n - (r + 1));

        // Group2a: p_i > u and p_j > u
        int count_x = st.query_gt(0, r, u);
        int count_y_high = st.query_gt(r + 1, n - 1, u);
        long long sum_a = 1LL * count_x * count_y_high;
        int m = (l == 0) ? 0 : st.query_gt(0, l - 1, u);
        long long sum_b = C2(count_x) - C2(m);
        long long group2a = sum_a + sum_b;

        // Group2b: p_i < d and p_j < d
        int count_xb = st.query_lt(0, r, d);
        int count_yb_high = st.query_lt(r + 1, n - 1, d);
        long long sum_ab = 1LL * count_xb * count_yb_high;
        int mb = (l == 0) ? 0 : st.query_lt(0, l - 1, d);
        long long sum_bb = C2(count_xb) - C2(mb);
        long long group2b = sum_ab + sum_bb;

        // Group2ab: u < p_i < d and u < p_j < d
        long long group2ab = 0;
        if (u < d) {
            int count_xab = st.query_between(0, r, u, d);
            int count_yab_high = st.query_between(r + 1, n - 1, u, d);
            long long sum_aab = 1LL * count_xab * count_yab_high;
            int mab = (l == 0) ? 0 : st.query_between(0, l - 1, u, d);
            long long sum_bab = C2(count_xab) - C2(mab);
            group2ab = sum_aab + sum_bab;
        }

        long long group2 = group2a + group2b - group2ab;
        long long non_overlapping = group1 + group2;
        long long total_pairs = C2(n);
        long long ans = total_pairs - non_overlapping;

        cout << ans << '\n';
    }

    return 0;
}<#sep#>-

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

typedef long long ll;
const int MOD = 1e9 + 7;
const int MAXB = 60;

vector<ll> pow2_i;

void precompute_pow2() {
    pow2_i.resize(61);
    pow2_i[0] = 1;
    for (int i = 1; i <= 60; ++i) {
        pow2_i[i] = (pow2_i[i-1] * 2) % MOD;
    }
}

void add_to_basis(vector<ll>& basis, ll x) {
    for (int i = MAXB; i >= 0; --i) {
        if (x & (1LL << i)) {
            if (basis[i] == 0) {
                basis[i] = x;
                break;
            } else {
                x ^= basis[i];
            }
        }
    }
}

int main() {
    ios_base::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(0);
    precompute_pow2();

    int n, m;
    cin >> n >> m;
    vector<vector<pair<int, ll>>> adj(n + 1);
    for (int i = 0; i < m; ++i) {
        int u, v;
        ll t;
        cin >> u >> v >> t;
        adj[u].emplace_back(v, t);
        adj[v].emplace_back(u, t);
    }

    vector<bool> visited(n + 1, false);
    vector<ll> xor_val(n + 1);
    vector<int> parent(n + 1, -1);
    ll total = 0;

    for (int node = 1; node <= n; ++node) {
        if (!visited[node]) {
            vector<ll> basis(MAXB + 1, 0);
            queue<int> q;
            q.push(node);
            visited[node] = true;
            xor_val[node] = 0;
            parent[node] = -1;
            vector<ll> xor_list;

            while (!q.empty()) {
                int u = q.front();
                q.pop();
                xor_list.push_back(xor_val[u]);

                for (auto [v, t] : adj[u]) {
                    if (!visited[v]) {
                        visited[v] = true;
                        xor_val[v] = xor_val[u] ^ t;
                        parent[v] = u;
                        q.push(v);
                    } else if (parent[u] != v) {
                        ll cycle_xor = xor_val[u] ^ t ^ xor_val[v];
                        add_to_basis(basis, cycle_xor);
                    }
                }
            }

            int C = xor_list.size();
            if (C < 2) continue;

            vector<bool> has_bit(MAXB + 1, false);
            int k = 0;
            for (int i = 0; i <= MAXB; ++i) {
                if (basis[i] != 0) {
                    has_bit[i] = true;
                    k++;
                }
            }

            ll pow2k = 1;
            for (int i = 0; i < k; ++i) {
                pow2k = (pow2k * 2) % MOD;
            }
            ll pow2k_half = (pow2k * 500000004LL) % MOD;

            ll c_choose_2 = ((C % MOD) * ((C - 1) % MOD)) % MOD;
            c_choose_2 = (c_choose_2 * 500000004LL) % MOD;

            for (int i = 0; i <= MAXB; ++i) {
                if (has_bit[i]) {
                    ll contribution = (c_choose_2 * pow2k_half) % MOD;
                    contribution = (contribution * pow2_i[i]) % MOD;
                    total = (total + contribution) % MOD;
                } else {
                    int cnt = 0;
                    for (ll x : xor_list) {
                        if (x & (1LL << i)) cnt++;
                    }
                    ll temp = ((cnt % MOD) * ((C - cnt) % MOD)) % MOD;
                    ll contribution = (temp * pow2k) % MOD;
                    contribution = (contribution * pow2_i[i]) % MOD;
                    total = (total + contribution) % MOD;
                }
            }
        }
    }

    cout << total % MOD << endl;
    return 0;
}<#sep#>-

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

using ll = long long;
const ll MOD = 1e18 + 3;
const ll BASE = 9999991;

vector<vector<int>> adj;
map<vector<ll>, ll> memo;
ll hash_counter = 0;

ll combine_hash(const vector<ll>& vec) {
    ll res = 0;
    for (ll x : vec) {
        res = (res * BASE + x) % MOD;
    }
    return res;
}

ll compute_hash(int u, int parent) {
    vector<ll> child_hashes;
    for (int v : adj[u]) {
        if (v != parent) {
            child_hashes.push_back(compute_hash(v, u));
        }
    }
    sort(child_hashes.begin(), child_hashes.end());
    if (!memo.count(child_hashes)) {
        memo[child_hashes] = ++hash_counter;
    }
    return memo[child_hashes];
}

vector<ll> hash_root;
vector<unordered_map<int, ll>> dp;

void dfs_reroot(int u, int parent) {
    vector<ll> child_hashes;
    for (int v : adj[u]) {
        if (v != parent) {
            child_hashes.push_back(hash_root[v]);
        }
    }
    if (parent != -1) {
        child_hashes.push_back(dp[u][parent]);
    }
    sort(child_hashes.begin(), child_hashes.end());
    if (!memo.count(child_hashes)) {
        memo[child_hashes] = ++hash_counter;
    }
    ll current_hash = memo[child_hashes];
    hash_root[u] = current_hash;

    for (int v : adj[u]) {
        if (v == parent) continue;
        vector<ll> new_child_hashes;
        for (int w : adj[u]) {
            if (w != v && w != parent) {
                new_child_hashes.push_back(hash_root[w]);
            }
        }
        if (parent != -1) {
            new_child_hashes.push_back(dp[u][parent]);
        }
        sort(new_child_hashes.begin(), new_child_hashes.end());
        if (!memo.count(new_child_hashes)) {
            memo[new_child_hashes] = ++hash_counter;
        }
        ll new_parent_hash = memo[new_child_hashes];
        dp[v][u] = new_parent_hash;
        dfs_reroot(v, u);
    }
}

int main() {
    ios::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(0);

    int n;
    cin >> n;
    adj.resize(n + 1);
    dp.resize(n + 1);
    hash_root.resize(n + 1, 0);

    for (int i = 0; i < n - 1; ++i) {
        int u, v;
        cin >> u >> v;
        adj[u].push_back(v);
        adj[v].push_back(u);
    }

    compute_hash(1, -1);

    for (int u = 1; u <= n; ++u) {
        for (int v : adj[u]) {
            dp[u][v] = compute_hash(v, u);
        }
    }

    hash_root[1] = compute_hash(1, -1);
    dfs_reroot(1, -1);

    vector<unordered_map<ll, int>> cnt(n + 1);
    for (int u = 1; u <= n; ++u) {
        vector<ll> hashes;
        function<void(int, int)> dfs = [&](int node, int parent) {
            vector<ll> child_hashes;
            for (int v : adj[node]) {
                if (v != parent) {
                    child_hashes.push_back(dp[node][v]);
                }
            }
            if (parent != -1) {
                child_hashes.push_back(dp[node][parent]);
            }
            sort(child_hashes.begin(), child_hashes.end());
            ll h = memo[child_hashes];
            cnt[u][h]++;
            for (int v : adj[node]) {
                if (v != parent) {
                    dfs(v, node);
                }
            }
        };
        dfs(u, -1);
    }

    int max_count = -1, ans = 1;
    for (int u = 1; u <= n; ++u) {
        int current = cnt[u].size();
        if (current > max_count) {
            max_count = current;
            ans = u;
        }
    }

    cout << ans << endl;

    return 0;
}<#sep#>-

#include <iostream>
#include <string>
#include <queue>
#include <unordered_set>
#include <algorithm>
#include <tuple>
#include <functional>

using namespace std;

bool isSubseq(const string &sub, const string &s) {
    int i = 0;
    for (char c : s) {
        if (i < sub.size() && c == sub[i]) {
            i++;
        }
    }
    return i == sub.size();
}

string addStrings(const string &a, const string &b) {
    int i = a.size() - 1;
    int j = b.size() - 1;
    int carry = 0;
    string result;
    while (i >= 0 || j >= 0 || carry > 0) {
        int sum = carry;
        if (i >= 0) sum += a[i--] - '0';
        if (j >= 0) sum += b[j--] - '0';
        carry = sum / 10;
        result.push_back((sum % 10) + '0');
    }
    reverse(result.begin(), result.end());
    return result;
}

vector<string> generateInsertions(const string &s) {
    vector<string> res;
    int n = s.size();
    for (int i = 0; i <= n; ++i) {
        for (char d = '0'; d <= '9'; ++d) {
            if (i == 0 && d == '0') continue;
            string new_s;
            if (i == 0) {
                new_s = string(1, d) + s;
            } else {
                new_s = s.substr(0, i) + string(1, d) + s.substr(i);
            }
            if (new_s.empty() || new_s[0] == '0') continue;
            res.push_back(new_s);
        }
    }
    sort(res.begin(), res.end());
    res.erase(unique(res.begin(), res.end()), res.end());
    return res;
}

struct State {
    int total_length;
    string x, y;
    State(int tl, string x_, string y_) : total_length(tl), x(x_), y(y_) {}
    bool operator<(const State &other) const {
        return total_length > other.total_length;
    }
};

string solve() {
    string equation;
    cin >> equation;
    size_t plus = equation.find('+');
    size_t equals = equation.find('=');
    string a_str = equation.substr(0, plus);
    string b_str = equation.substr(plus+1, equals - plus -1);
    string c_str = equation.substr(equals+1);
    
    string z_initial = addStrings(a_str, b_str);
    if (z_initial == c_str) {
        return a_str + "+" + b_str + "=" + c_str;
    }
    
    priority_queue<State> pq;
    unordered_set<string> visited;
    
    auto add_state = [&](const string &x, const string &y) {
        string key = x + "," + y;
        if (visited.count(key)) return;
        visited.insert(key);
        string z = addStrings(x, y);
        int total_len = x.size() + y.size() + z.size() + 2;
        pq.emplace(total_len, x, y);
    };
    
    add_state(a_str, b_str);
    
    while (!pq.empty()) {
        auto current = pq.top();
        pq.pop();
        string x = current.x;
        string y = current.y;
        string z = addStrings(x, y);
        
        if (isSubseq(c_str, z)) {
            return x + "+" + y + "=" + z;
        }
        
        vector<string> x_insertions = generateInsertions(x);
        for (const string &new_x : x_insertions) {
            add_state(new_x, y);
        }
        
        vector<string> y_insertions = generateInsertions(y);
        for (const string &new_y : y_insertions) {
            add_state(x, new_y);
        }
    }
    
    return "";
}

int main() {
    cout << solve() << endl;
    return 0;
}<#sep#>-

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

const int MAXN = 2e5 + 5;
const int LOG = 20;

struct Edge {
    int u, v, w, idx;
    Edge() {}
    Edge(int u, int v, int w, int idx) : u(u), v(v), w(w), idx(idx) {}
    bool operator<(const Edge& other) const {
        return w < other.w;
    }
};

vector<Edge> original_edges;
vector<bool> in_mst;
int parent_dsu[MAXN];
int up[LOG][MAXN];
int max_edge[LOG][MAXN];
int depth[MAXN];
int parent_tree[MAXN];
int edge_weight_tree[MAXN];
vector<pair<int, int>> mst_adj[MAXN];
int n, m;

int find_dsu(int u) {
    if (parent_dsu[u] != u) {
        parent_dsu[u] = find_dsu(parent_dsu[u]);
    }
    return parent_dsu[u];
}

bool unite_dsu(int u, int v) {
    u = find_dsu(u);
    v = find_dsu(v);
    if (u == v) return false;
    parent_dsu[u] = v;
    return true;
}

void preprocess_lca() {
    for (int i = 1; i <= n; ++i) {
        up[0][i] = parent_tree[i];
        max_edge[0][i] = edge_weight_tree[i];
    }
    for (int k = 1; k < LOG; ++k) {
        for (int i = 1; i <= n; ++i) {
            up[k][i] = up[k-1][ up[k-1][i] ];
            max_edge[k][i] = max( max_edge[k-1][i], max_edge[k-1][ up[k-1][i] ] );
        }
    }
}

int get_max_edge(int u, int v) {
    if (u == v) return 0;
    int max_e = 0;
    if (depth[u] < depth[v]) swap(u, v);
    for (int k = LOG-1; k >= 0; --k) {
        if (depth[u] - (1 << k) >= depth[v]) {
            max_e = max(max_e, max_edge[k][u]);
            u = up[k][u];
        }
    }
    if (u == v) return max_e;
    for (int k = LOG-1; k >= 0; --k) {
        if (up[k][u] != up[k][v]) {
            max_e = max(max_e, max_edge[k][u]);
            max_e = max(max_e, max_edge[k][v]);
            u = up[k][u];
            v = up[k][v];
        }
    }
    max_e = max(max_e, max_edge[0][u]);
    max_e = max(max_e, max_edge[0][v]);
    return max_e;
}

int main() {
    ios_base::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(0);

    cin >> n >> m;
    original_edges.resize(m);
    in_mst.assign(m, false);

    for (int i = 0; i < m; ++i) {
        int u, v, w;
        cin >> u >> v >> w;
        original_edges[i] = Edge(u, v, w, i);
    }

    vector<Edge> sorted_edges = original_edges;
    sort(sorted_edges.begin(), sorted_edges.end());

    for (int i = 1; i <= n; ++i) parent_dsu[i] = i;

    int mst_total = 0;
    for (const Edge& e : sorted_edges) {
        if (unite_dsu(e.u, e.v)) {
            mst_total += e.w;
            in_mst[e.idx] = true;
            mst_adj[e.u].emplace_back(e.v, e.w);
            mst_adj[e.v].emplace_back(e.u, e.w);
        }
    }

    fill(parent_tree, parent_tree + n + 1, -1);
    queue<int> q;
    q.push(1);
    parent_tree[1] = 0;
    depth[1] = 0;
    while (!q.empty()) {
        int u = q.front();
        q.pop();
        for (auto [v, w] : mst_adj[u]) {
            if (parent_tree[v] == -1 && v != parent_tree[u]) {
                parent_tree[v] = u;
                edge_weight_tree[v] = w;
                depth[v] = depth[u] + 1;
                q.push(v);
            }
        }
    }

    preprocess_lca();

    for (const Edge& e : original_edges) {
        if (in_mst[e.idx]) {
            cout << mst_total << '\n';
        } else {
            int max_e = get_max_edge(e.u, e.v);
            cout << (mst_total - max_e + e.w) << '\n';
        }
    }

    return 0;
}<#sep#>-

#include <iostream>
#include <vector>
#include <cmath>
#include <iomanip>

using namespace std;

struct Circle {
    double x, y, r;
};

const double EPS = 1e-8;

vector<pair<double, double>> solve_apollonius_intersection(const Circle& c1, const Circle& c2, const Circle& c3) {
    vector<pair<double, double>> candidates;

    // Compute Apollonius circles for pairs (1-2) and (1-3)
    double r1 = c1.r, r2 = c2.r, r3 = c3.r;
    double x1 = c1.x, y1 = c1.y, x2 = c2.x, y2 = c2.y, x3 = c3.x, y3 = c3.y;

    // Pair 1-2
    double A1 = r2*r2 - r1*r1;
    double C1 = 2 * (r1*r1 * x2 - r2*r2 * x1);
    double D1 = 2 * (r1*r1 * y2 - r2*r2 * y1);
    double E1 = r2*r2*(x1*x1 + y1*y1) - r1*r1*(x2*x2 + y2*y2);

    // Pair 1-3
    double A2 = r3*r3 - r1*r1;
    double C2 = 2 * (r1*r1 * x3 - r3*r3 * x1);
    double D2 = 2 * (r1*r1 * y3 - r3*r3 * y1);
    double E2 = r3*r3*(x1*x1 + y1*y1) - r1*r1*(x3*x3 + y3*y3);

    // Check if both Apollonius circles are lines (A1 and A2 are zero)
    if (A1 == 0 && A2 == 0) {
        // Solve the two line equations C1x + D1y + E1 = 0 and C2x + D2y + E2 = 0
        double det = C1 * D2 - C2 * D1;
        if (det != 0) {
            double x = (D1 * E2 - D2 * E1) / det;
            double y = (C2 * E1 - C1 * E2) / det;
            candidates.emplace_back(x, y);
        }
        return candidates;
    }

    // Compute radical line coefficients
    double a = A1*C2 - A2*C1;
    double b = A1*D2 - A2*D1;
    double c = A1*E2 - A2*E1;

    // Handle the case where radical line is invalid
    if (a == 0 && b == 0) {
        return candidates;
    }

    // Solve for intersection points
    if (b != 0) {
        // Express y in terms of x: y = (-a x - c) / b
        double coeff_x2 = A1 * (a*a + b*b);
        double coeff_x = 2*A1*a*c + C1*b*b - D1*a*b;
        double coeff_const = A1*c*c - D1*b*c + E1*b*b;

        if (coeff_x2 == 0) {
            if (coeff_x == 0) {
                if (coeff_const == 0) {
                    // Infinite solutions (unlikely given problem constraints)
                }
            } else {
                double x = -coeff_const / coeff_x;
                double y = (-a*x - c)/b;
                candidates.emplace_back(x, y);
            }
        } else {
            double discriminant = coeff_x*coeff_x - 4*coeff_x2*coeff_const;
            if (discriminant >= 0) {
                double sqrt_d = sqrt(discriminant);
                double x1 = (-coeff_x + sqrt_d) / (2 * coeff_x2);
                double y1 = (-a*x1 - c)/b;
                candidates.emplace_back(x1, y1);
                double x2 = (-coeff_x - sqrt_d) / (2 * coeff_x2);
                double y2 = (-a*x2 - c)/b;
                candidates.emplace_back(x2, y2);
            }
        }
    } else {
        // b == 0, line is x = -c/a
        double x_val = -c / a;
        // Substitute into first Apollonius equation
        double A = A1;
        double B = D1;
        double C_term = A1*x_val*x_val + C1*x_val + E1;
        if (A == 0) {
            if (B != 0) {
                double y_val = -C_term / B;
                candidates.emplace_back(x_val, y_val);
            }
        } else {
            double discriminant = B*B - 4*A*C_term;
            if (discriminant >= 0) {
                double sqrt_d = sqrt(discriminant);
                double y1 = (-B + sqrt_d) / (2*A);
                double y2 = (-B - sqrt_d) / (2*A);
                candidates.emplace_back(x_val, y1);
                candidates.emplace_back(x_val, y2);
            }
        }
    }

    return candidates;
}

int main() {
    Circle circles[3];
    for (int i = 0; i < 3; ++i) {
        cin >> circles[i].x >> circles[i].y >> circles[i].r;
    }

    vector<pair<double, double>> candidates = solve_apollonius_intersection(circles[0], circles[1], circles[2]);

    vector<pair<double, double>> valid_points;
    for (auto& p : candidates) {
        bool valid = true;
        double angle = -1;
        for (int i = 0; i < 3; ++i) {
            const Circle& c = circles[i];
            double dx = p.first - c.x;
            double dy = p.second - c.y;
            double d = sqrt(dx*dx + dy*dy);
            if (d < c.r - EPS) {
                valid = false;
                break;
            }
            double s = c.r / d;
            if (s > 1.0) {
                valid = false;
                break;
            }
            double a = 2 * asin(s);
            if (i == 0) {
                angle = a;
            } else if (abs(angle - a) > 1e-8) {
                valid = false;
                break;
            }
        }
        if (valid) {
            valid_points.push_back(p);
        }
    }

    if (!valid_points.empty()) {
        int best_idx = 0;
        double best_angle = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < valid_points.size(); ++i) {
            const auto& p = valid_points[i];
            const Circle& c = circles[0];
            double dx = p.first - c.x;
            double dy = p.second - c.y;
            double d = sqrt(dx*dx + dy*dy);
            double s = c.r / d;
            double angle = 2 * asin(s);
            if (i == 0 || angle > best_angle) {
                best_angle = angle;
                best_idx = i;
            }
        }
        const auto& best = valid_points[best_idx];
        cout << fixed << setprecision(5) << best.first << " " << best.second << endl;
    }

    return 0;
}<#sep#>-

#include <iostream>
#include <vector>
#include <string>

using namespace std;

int main() {
    int N, K;
    cin >> N >> K;
    string P;
    cin >> P;
    string mask_str;
    cin >> mask_str;

    vector<char> S(N, '\0');

    // Step 1: Enforce '1's in the mask and check conflicts
    for (int i = 0; i < mask_str.size(); ++i) {
        if (mask_str[i] == '1') {
            int start = i;
            for (int j = 0; j < P.size(); ++j) {
                int pos = start + j;
                if (pos >= N) {
                    cout << "No solution" << endl;
                    return 0;
                }
                if (S[pos] == '\0') {
                    S[pos] = P[j];
                } else {
                    if (S[pos] != P[j]) {
                        cout << "No solution" << endl;
                        return 0;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    // Step 2: Check forbidden '0's for existing P
    for (int i = 0; i < mask_str.size(); ++i) {
        if (mask_str[i] == '0') {
            int start = i;
            bool matches = true;
            for (int j = 0; j < P.size(); ++j) {
                int pos = start + j;
                if (pos >= N) {
                    matches = false;
                    break;
                }
                if (S[pos] == '\0') {
                    matches = false;
                    break;
                }
                if (S[pos] != P[j]) {
                    matches = false;
                    break;
                }
            }
            if (matches) {
                cout << "No solution" << endl;
                return 0;
            }
        }
    }

    // Step 3: Process forbidden '0's to prevent P
    for (int i = 0; i < mask_str.size(); ++i) {
        if (mask_str[i] == '0') {
            int start = i;
            bool all_fixed_match = true;
            for (int j = 0; j < P.size(); ++j) {
                int pos = start + j;
                if (pos >= N) {
                    all_fixed_match = false;
                    break;
                }
                if (S[pos] == '\0') {
                    all_fixed_match = false;
                    break;
                }
                if (S[pos] != P[j]) {
                    all_fixed_match = false;
                    break;
                }
            }
            if (!all_fixed_match) continue;

            bool found = false;
            for (int j = 0; j < P.size(); ++j) {
                int pos = start + j;
                if (pos >= N) continue;
                if (S[pos] == '\0') {
                    char desired = P[j];
                    for (char c = 'a'; c < 'a' + K; ++c) {
                        if (c != desired) {
                            S[pos] = c;
                            found = true;
                            break;
                        }
                    }
                    if (found) break;
                }
            }
            if (!found) {
                cout << "No solution" << endl;
                return 0;
            }
        }
    }

    // Fill remaining positions with 'a' (or any valid character)
    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
        if (S[i] == '\0') {
            S[i] = 'a';
        }
    }

    // Output the result
    for (char c : S) {
        cout << c;
    }
    cout << endl;

    return 0;
}<#sep#>-

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

int main() {
    string s;
    cin >> s;
    int n = s.size();
    vector<unordered_set<char>> allowed(n);
    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        allowed[i] = {'a','b','c','d','e','f'};
    }
    int m;
    cin >> m;
    for (int k = 0; k < m; ++k) {
        int pos;
        string chars;
        cin >> pos >> chars;
        pos--;
        allowed[pos].clear();
        for (char c : chars) {
            allowed[pos].insert(c);
        }
    }
    vector<int> original_counts(6, 0);
    for (char c : s) {
        original_counts[c - 'a']++;
    }
    vector<vector<int>> prefix(6, vector<int>(n, 0));
    for (int d = 0; d < 6; ++d) {
        char c = 'a' + d;
        int sum = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
            if (allowed[i].count(c)) {
                sum++;
            }
            prefix[d][i] = sum;
        }
    }
    vector<int> available_initial(6);
    for (int d = 0; d < 6; ++d) {
        available_initial[d] = prefix[d][n-1];
    }
    bool possible = true;
    for (int d = 0; d < 6; ++d) {
        if (available_initial[d] < original_counts[d]) {
            possible = false;
            break;
        }
    }
    if (!possible) {
        cout << "Impossible" << endl;
        return 0;
    }
    vector<int> current_used(6, 0);
    string result;
    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        vector<char> candidates;
        for (char c : {'a','b','c','d','e','f'}) {
            if (allowed[i].count(c)) {
                candidates.push_back(c);
            }
        }
        sort(candidates.begin(), candidates.end());
        bool found = false;
        for (char c : candidates) {
            int d = c - 'a';
            if (current_used[d] >= original_counts[d]) continue;
            bool ok = true;
            for (int d_check = 0; d_check < 6; ++d_check) {
                int required = original_counts[d_check] - current_used[d_check];
                if (d_check == d) required--;
                int available_remaining = available_initial[d_check] - prefix[d_check][i];
                if (available_remaining < required) {
                    ok = false;
                    break;
                }
            }
            if (ok) {
                current_used[d]++;
                result += c;
                found = true;
                break;
            }
        }
        if (!found) {
            cout << "Impossible" << endl;
            return 0;
        }
    }
    cout << result << endl;
    return 0;
}<#sep#>-

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

struct DSU {
    vector<int> parent;
    vector<int> size;
    vector<int> diameter;

    DSU(int n) : parent(n+1), size(n+1, 1), diameter(n+1, 0) {
        iota(parent.begin(), parent.end(), 0);
    }

    int find(int x) {
        if (parent[x] != x) {
            parent[x] = find(parent[x]);
        }
        return parent[x];
    }

    void unite(int x, int y) {
        x = find(x);
        y = find(y);
        if (x == y) return;
        if (size[x] < size[y]) swap(x, y);
        parent[y] = x;
        size[x] += size[y];
        diameter[x] = max({diameter[x], diameter[y], (diameter[x] + 1)/2 + (diameter[y] + 1)/2 + 1});
    }
};

int main() {
    ios_base::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(nullptr);

    int n, m, q;
    cin >> n >> m >> q;

    vector<vector<int>> adj(n+1);
    DSU dsu(n);

    for (int i = 0; i < m; ++i) {
        int a, b;
        cin >> a >> b;
        adj[a].push_back(b);
        adj[b].push_back(a);
        dsu.unite(a, b);
    }

    vector<bool> visited(n+1, false);
    for (int i = 1; i <= n; ++i) {
        if (dsu.find(i) == i && !visited[i]) {
            queue<int> q;
            q.push(i);
            visited[i] = true;
            vector<int> component;
            while (!q.empty()) {
                int u = q.front();
                q.pop();
                component.push_back(u);
                for (int v : adj[u]) {
                    if (!visited[v]) {
                        visited[v] = true;
                        q.push(v);
                    }
                }
            }
            if (component.size() == 1) {
                dsu.diameter[i] = 0;
                continue;
            }
            int u = component[0], max_dist = 0;
            queue<pair<int, int>> q_bfs;
            vector<bool> visited_bfs(n+1, false);
            q_bfs.push({u, 0});
            visited_bfs[u] = true;
            while (!q_bfs.empty()) {
                auto [current, dist] = q_bfs.front();
                q_bfs.pop();
                if (dist > max_dist) {
                    max_dist = dist;
                    u = current;
                }
                for (int v : adj[current]) {
                    if (!visited_bfs[v]) {
                        visited_bfs[v] = true;
                        q_bfs.push({v, dist+1});
                    }
                }
            }
            max_dist = 0;
            visited_bfs.assign(n+1, false);
            q_bfs.push({u, 0});
            visited_bfs[u] = true;
            while (!q_bfs.empty()) {
                auto [current, dist] = q_bfs.front();
                q_bfs.pop();
                max_dist = max(max_dist, dist);
                for (int v : adj[current]) {
                    if (!visited_bfs[v]) {
                        visited_bfs[v] = true;
                        q_bfs.push({v, dist+1});
                    }
                }
            }
            dsu.diameter[i] = max_dist;
        }
    }

    while (q--) {
        int type;
        cin >> type;
        if (type == 1) {
            int x;
            cin >> x;
            cout << dsu.diameter[dsu.find(x)] << '\n';
        } else {
            int x, y;
            cin >> x >> y;
            dsu.unite(x, y);
        }
    }

    return 0;
}<#sep#>-

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

const int MOD = 998244353;
const int MAXN = 3000;

vector<long long> fact(MAXN + 1), inv_fact(MAXN + 1);

void precompute() {
    fact[0] = 1;
    for (int i = 1; i <= MAXN; ++i)
        fact[i] = fact[i-1] * i % MOD;
    inv_fact[MAXN] = 1;
    long long base = fact[MAXN], exp = MOD - 2;
    while (exp) {
        if (exp & 1) inv_fact[MAXN] = inv_fact[MAXN] * base % MOD;
        base = base * base % MOD;
        exp >>= 1;
    }
    for (int i = MAXN - 1; i >= 0; --i)
        inv_fact[i] = inv_fact[i+1] * (i+1) % MOD;
}

long long comb(int n, int k) {
    if (k < 0 || k > n) return 0;
    return fact[n] * inv_fact[k] % MOD * inv_fact[n - k] % MOD;
}

int main() {
    precompute();
    int n, c;
    cin >> n >> c;
    vector<int> a(n);
    for (int &x : a) cin >> x;

    vector<int> cnt(c + 1, 0);
    for (int x : a) cnt[x]++;

    bool all_present = true;
    for (int x = 1; x <= c; ++x)
        if (!cnt[x]) { all_present = false; break; }

    if (!all_present) {
        long long total = 1;
        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) total = total * 2 % MOD;
        total = (total - 1 + MOD) % MOD;
        cout << total << " ";
        for (int p = 1; p <= n; ++p) cout << "0 ";
        cout << '\n';
        return 0;
    }

    vector<vector<long long>> prefix_sums(c + 1);
    vector<long long> pow2(c + 1);
    for (int x = 1; x <= c; ++x) {
        int cx = cnt[x];
        pow2[x] = 1;
        for (int i = 0; i < cx; ++i)
            pow2[x] = pow2[x] * 2 % MOD;
        vector<long long> ps(cx + 1);
        ps[0] = comb(cx, 0);
        for (int k = 1; k <= cx; ++k)
            ps[k] = (ps[k-1] + comb(cx, k)) % MOD;
        prefix_sums[x] = ps;
    }

    long long total_all = 1;
    for (int x = 1; x <= c; ++x) {
        long long term = (pow2[x] - 1 + MOD) % MOD;
        total_all = total_all * term % MOD;
    }
    long long pow2n = 1;
    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) pow2n = pow2n * 2 % MOD;
    long long s0 = (pow2n - 1 - total_all + 2 * MOD) % MOD;
    vector<long long> s(n + 1, 0);
    s[0] = s0;

    int min_cnt = *min_element(cnt.begin() + 1, cnt.end());

    for (int p = 1; p <= min_cnt; ++p) {
        long long fp = 1;
        for (int x = 1; x <= c; ++x) {
            int cx = cnt[x];
            if (cx < p) { fp = 0; break; }
            long long sum = (pow2[x] - (p ? prefix_sums[x][p-1] : 0) + MOD) % MOD;
            fp = fp * sum % MOD;
        }
        long long fp1 = 0;
        if (p + 1 <= min_cnt) {
            fp1 = 1;
            for (int x = 1; x <= c; ++x) {
                int cx = cnt[x];
                if (cx < p + 1) { fp1 = 0; break; }
                long long sum = (pow2[x] - prefix_sums[x][p] + MOD) % MOD;
                fp1 = fp1 * sum % MOD;
            }
        }
        s[p] = (fp - fp1 + MOD) % MOD;
    }

    for (int p = 0; p <= n; ++p)
        cout << s[p] << " ";
    cout << '\n';
    return 0;
}<#sep#>-

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

typedef long long ll;

struct Edge {
    int to, rev;
    ll cap, cost;
};

vector<vector<Edge>> g;
vector<ll> dist;
vector<int> prevv, preve;
const ll INF = 1e18;

void add_edge(int from, int to, ll cap, ll cost) {
    g[from].push_back({to, (int)g[to].size(), cap, cost});
    g[to].push_back({from, (int)g[from].size()-1, 0, -cost});
}

ll min_cost_flow(int s, int t, ll f) {
    ll res = 0;
    vector<ll> h(g.size(), 0);
    while (f > 0) {
        priority_queue<pair<ll, int>, vector<pair<ll, int>>, greater<pair<ll, int>>> pq;
        fill(dist.begin(), dist.end(), INF);
        dist[s] = 0;
        pq.push({0, s});
        while (!pq.empty()) {
            auto [d, v] = pq.top(); pq.pop();
            if (dist[v] < d) continue;
            for (size_t i = 0; i < g[v].size(); i++) {
                Edge &e = g[v][i];
                if (e.cap > 0 && dist[e.to] > d + e.cost + h[v] - h[e.to]) {
                    dist[e.to] = d + e.cost + h[v] - h[e.to];
                    prevv[e.to] = v;
                    preve[e.to] = i;
                    pq.push({dist[e.to], e.to});
                }
            }
        }
        if (dist[t] == INF) return -1;
        for (size_t v = 0; v < h.size(); v++) h[v] += dist[v];
        ll d = f;
        for (int v = t; v != s; v = prevv[v]) {
            d = min(d, g[prevv[v]][preve[v]].cap);
        }
        f -= d;
        res += d * h[t];
        for (int v = t; v != s; v = prevv[v]) {
            Edge &e = g[prevv[v]][preve[v]];
            e.cap -= d;
            g[v][e.rev].cap += d;
        }
    }
    return res;
}

int main() {
    ios::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(0);

    int n, m;
    cin >> n >> m;
    vector<string> s(n);
    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        cin >> s[i];
    }
    vector<vector<int>> a(n, vector<int>(m));
    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (int j = 0; j < m; ++j) {
            cin >> a[i][j];
        }
    }

    int num_jc = m * 26;
    int source = 0;
    int sink = n + num_jc + 1;
    int offset = n + 1;

    g.resize(sink + 1);
    prevv.resize(sink + 1);
    preve.resize(sink + 1);
    dist.resize(sink + 1);

    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        add_edge(source, i + 1, 1, 0);
    }

    for (int j = 0; j < m; ++j) {
        for (char c = 'a'; c <= 'z'; ++c) {
            int jc_idx = j * 26 + (c - 'a');
